import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:flutter_inappwebview/flutter_inappwebview.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';

import 'screens/dashboard_screen.dart';
import 'core/app_theme.dart';

bool globalDisableAutoCacheClear = false;
void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  SystemChrome.setSystemUIOverlayStyle(const SystemUiOverlayStyle(
      statusBarColor: Colors.transparent,
      statusBarIconBrightness: Brightness.dark,
      systemNavigationBarColor: Colors.transparent,
      systemNavigationBarIconBrightness: Brightness.dark));
  SystemChrome.setEnabledSystemUIMode(SystemUiMode.edgeToEdge);

  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final savedTheme = prefs.getString('app_theme_mode');
  if (savedTheme == 'light') {
    appThemeNotifier.value = ThemeMode.light;
  } else if (savedTheme == 'dark') {
    appThemeNotifier.value = ThemeMode.dark;
  }

  runApp(const CyberCanvasApp());
}

// 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・售tatefulWidget縺ｫ螟画峩縺励√Λ繧､繝輔し繧､繧ｯ繝ｫ・医い繝励Μ縺ｮ迥ｶ諷具ｼ峨ｒ逶｣隕悶〒縺阪ｋ繧医≧縺ｫ縺吶ｋ
class CyberCanvasApp extends StatefulWidget {
  const CyberCanvasApp({super.key});

  @override
  State<CyberCanvasApp> createState() => _CyberCanvasAppState();
}

// 笘・WidgetsBindingObserver 繧定ｿｽ蜉縺励※縲√ヰ繝・け繧ｰ繝ｩ繧ｦ繝ｳ繝臥ｧｻ陦後ｒ讀懃衍縺吶ｋ
class _CyberCanvasAppState extends State<CyberCanvasApp> with WidgetsBindingObserver {

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // 繧｢繝励Μ襍ｷ蜍墓凾縺ｫ逶｣隕悶ｒ髢句ｧ・
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    // 襍ｷ蜍墓凾縺ｫ繧ょｿｵ縺ｮ縺溘ａ荳蠎ｦ縺頑祉髯､
    _clearAppCache();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    // 繧｢繝励Μ邨ゆｺ・凾縺ｫ逶｣隕悶ｒ隗｣髯､
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    super.dispose();
  }

  // 笘・縺薙％縺梧､懃衍繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ・壹後い繝励Μ縺瑚ｦ九∴縺ｪ縺上↑縺｣縺溘咲ｭ峨〒蜻ｼ縺ｰ繧後ｋ
  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    super.didChangeAppLifecycleState(state);

    if (state == AppLifecycleState.hidden || state == AppLifecycleState.paused) {
      // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壹き繝｡繝ｩ襍ｷ蜍穂ｸｭ縺ｪ縺ｩ縺ｧ繝輔Λ繧ｰ縺檎ｫ九▲縺ｦ縺・ｌ縺ｰ縲√♀謗・勁繧偵せ繧ｭ繝・・縺励※邨ゆｺ・☆繧・
      if (globalDisableAutoCacheClear) {
        debugPrint("Auto Cache Clear Skipped (Camera in use)");
        return;
      }

      _clearAppCache();
    }
  }

  // 笘・繝舌ャ繧ｯ繧ｰ繝ｩ繧ｦ繝ｳ繝峨〒螳溯｡後＆繧後ｋ閾ｪ蜍輔♀謗・勁繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ
  // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壹ヰ繝・け繧ｰ繝ｩ繧ｦ繝ｳ繝峨〒螳溯｡後＆繧後ｋ閾ｪ蜍輔♀謗・勁繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ
  Future<void> _clearAppCache() async {
    if (globalDisableAutoCacheClear) return;
    // ----------------------------------------------------
    // 縲仙炎髯､縲・
    // 莉･荳九・2陦後ｒ螳溯｡後☆繧九→Canvas縺ｮSessionStorage縺梧ｶ医∴縲・
    // Okta縺ｧ縺・■縺・■繝ｭ繧ｰ繧､繝ｳ繧呈ｱゅａ繧峨ｌ繧九◆繧∝炎髯､縺励∪縺励◆縲・
    // final webStorageManager = WebStorageManager.instance();
    // await webStorageManager.deleteAllData();
    // ----------------------------------------------------

    try {
      // OS縺ｮ荳譎ゅョ繧｣繝ｬ繧ｯ繝医Μ・・emp・峨↓縺ゅｋ荳崎ｦ√↑繝輔ぃ繧､繝ｫ繧剃ｸ謗・
      // 蜍慕判繝舌ャ繝輔ぃ繧・判蜒上・谿矩ｪｸ・・GB縺ｮ蜈・・・峨・縺薙ｌ縺ｧ遒ｺ螳溘↓豸医∴縺ｾ縺・
      final tempDir = await getTemporaryDirectory();
      if (tempDir.existsSync()) {
        tempDir.listSync().forEach((file) {
          try {
            if (file is File) {
              file.deleteSync();
            }
          } catch (_) {}
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Auto Cache Clear Error (Temp files): $e");
    }

    debugPrint("笨ｨ Auto App Cache Cleared (Storage optimized, Session kept)");
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ValueListenableBuilder<ThemeMode>(
        valueListenable: appThemeNotifier,
        builder: (_, currentMode, __) {
          return MaterialApp(
              title: 'k.portal',
              debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
              themeMode: currentMode,
              theme: ThemeData(
                  brightness: Brightness.light,
                  scaffoldBackgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF5F5F7)),
              darkTheme: ThemeData(
                  brightness: Brightness.dark,
                  scaffoldBackgroundColor: Colors.black),
              home: const MainSkeleton());
        });
  }
}
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

//app_snackbar.dart

// 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・・OS縺ｮ繧｢繧ｯ繧ｻ繧ｷ繝薙Μ繝・ぅ險ｭ螳壹ｒ辟｡隕悶＠縺ｦ縲∝ｼｷ蛻ｶ逧・↓1.5遘偵〒豸医☆繧ｿ繧､繝槭・繧定ｿｽ蜉
class AppSnackBar {
  // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・夂樟蝨ｨ遞ｼ蜒堺ｸｭ縺ｮ繧ｿ繧､繝槭・繧剃ｿ晄戟縺励・㍾隍・ｮ溯｡後ｒ髦ｲ縺舌◆繧√・螟画焚
  static Timer? _hideTimer;

  static void showUndo(BuildContext context, String message, VoidCallback onUndo) {
    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);

    // 譌｢蟄倥・繧ｹ繝翫ャ繧ｯ繝舌・繧堤｢ｺ螳溘↓豸医＠縺ｦ縺九ｉ譁ｰ縺励＞繧ゅ・繧定｡ｨ遉ｺ
    messenger.clearSnackBars();

    // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・壻ｻ･蜑阪・繧ｿ繧､繝槭・縺悟虚縺・※縺・◆繧峨く繝｣繝ｳ繧ｻ繝ｫ・医Μ繧ｻ繝・ヨ・峨☆繧・
    _hideTimer?.cancel();

    const Color barBgColor = Colors.black;
    const Color vibrantRed = Color(0xFFFF1744);

    messenger.showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(
            message,
            style: const TextStyle(
                color: Colors.grey,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w300,
                fontSize: 13
            )
        ),
        backgroundColor: barBgColor,
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 2000),
        elevation: 0,
        margin: EdgeInsets.only(
            bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.bottom + 2.0,
            left: 8.0,
            right: 8.0
        ),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30)),
        action: SnackBarAction(
          label: "蜈・↓謌ｻ縺・,
          textColor: vibrantRed,
          onPressed: onUndo,
        ),
      ),
    );

    // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壹ち繧､繝槭・繧貞､画焚縺ｫ譬ｼ邏阪＠縲・遘貞ｾ後↓螳溯｡後る｣邯壼他縺ｳ蜃ｺ縺玲凾縺ｯ荳翫〒繧ｭ繝｣繝ｳ繧ｻ繝ｫ縺輔ｌ繧・
    _hideTimer = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 2000), () {
      try {
        messenger.hideCurrentSnackBar();
      } catch (_) {}
    });
  }

  static void showError(BuildContext context, String message) {
    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
    messenger.clearSnackBars();
    _hideTimer?.cancel(); // 繧ｨ繝ｩ繝ｼ陦ｨ遉ｺ譎ゅｂ蠢ｵ縺ｮ縺溘ａ繧ｿ繧､繝槭・繧偵Μ繧ｻ繝・ヨ

    messenger.showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Row(
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.error_outline, color: Color(0xFFD0312D), size: 20),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                message,
                style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Color(0xFFD0312D),
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w300,
                    fontSize: 13
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        backgroundColor: Colors.black,
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        duration: const Duration(seconds: 4),
        elevation: 0,
        margin: EdgeInsets.only(
            bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).padding.bottom + 80.0,
            left: 8.0,
            right: 8.0
        ),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30)),
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

//app_theme.dart

final ValueNotifier<ThemeMode> appThemeNotifier = ValueNotifier(ThemeMode.system);

class AppTheme {
  static const Color accent = Color(0xFFD0312D);
  static Color bg(BuildContext context) => Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFFF2F2F2);
  static Color panel(BuildContext context) => Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark ? const Color(0xFF1E1E1E) : const Color(0xFFE8E8E8);
  static Color textMain(BuildContext context) => Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark ? Colors.white : Colors.black87;
  static Color textSub(BuildContext context) => Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark ? Colors.white54 : Colors.black54;

  static Color getGradientColor(int index, int total) {
    if (total <= 1) return const Color(0xFF222222);
    final double t = index / (total > 15 ? 15 : total);
    final double adjustedT = (t % 1.0) * 0.85 + 0.1;
    return Color.lerp(const Color(0xFF101010), const Color(0xFFEEEEEE), adjustedT)!;
  }

  static Color getCustomCourseColor(String name) {
    int hash = name.hashCode;
    double lightness = 0.3 + (hash.abs() % 40) / 100.0;
    return HSLColor.fromAHSL(1.0, 0, 0, lightness).toColor();
  }

  static Color getTextColorForBackground(Color bg) => ThemeData.estimateBrightnessForColor(bg) == Brightness.light ? Colors.black : Colors.white;
}
