                    if (_schedulePageController.hasClients) {
                      _schedulePageController.animateToPage(10000, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300), curve: Curves.easeOutCubic);
                    }
                  },
                  child: Container(
                    height: 30,
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(color: btnBg, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15)),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: _currentSchedulePage > 10000
                          ? [Icon(Icons.arrow_back_ios_new, size: 10, color: btnIconColor), const SizedBox(width: 4), Text("${DateTime.now().month}/${DateTime.now().day}", style: TextStyle(color: btnIconColor, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]
                          : [Text("${DateTime.now().month}/${DateTime.now().day}", style: TextStyle(color: btnIconColor, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), const SizedBox(width: 4), Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios, size: 10, color: btnIconColor)],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              )
          )
      ],
    );
  }
  // 繝医げ繝ｫ繝懊ち繝ｳ逕ｨ繝倥Ν繝代・繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ
  // 繝医げ繝ｫ繝懊ち繝ｳ逕ｨ繝倥Ν繝代・繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ
  Widget _buildModeButton(String mode, Color tCol) {
    bool isSel = _scheduleMode == mode;
    return GestureDetector(
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      onTap: () {
        if (_scheduleMode != mode) {
          setState(() {
            _scheduleMode = mode;
            _currentSchedulePage = 10000; // 繝｢繝ｼ繝峨ｒ螟峨∴縺溘ｉ縲御ｻ翫阪↓謌ｻ縺・

            // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壹Δ繝ｼ繝牙・譖ｿ譎ゅｂPageController繧貞・譛滉ｽ咲ｽｮ(10000)縺ｧ譁ｰ縺励￥菴懊ｊ逶ｴ縺・
            final oldController = _schedulePageController;
            _schedulePageController = PageController(initialPage: 10000);
            Future.delayed(Duration.zero, () => oldController.dispose());

            if (_tasksScrollController.hasClients) _tasksScrollController.jumpTo(0.0);
            _tasksScrollOffset = 0.0;
          });
        }
      },
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8.0, vertical: 4.0),
        child: Text(
          mode,
          style: TextStyle(
            color: tCol.withOpacity(isSel ? 1.0 : 0.3),
            fontSize: 24,
            fontWeight: isSel ? FontWeight.w400 : FontWeight.w200,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
  // === 莉･荳九∵眠隕剰ｿｽ蜉縺吶ｋ謠冗判逕ｨ繝倥Ν繝代・繝｡繧ｽ繝・ラ3遞ｮ ===

  Widget _buildDayPage(DateTime pageDate, int index, double topPadding, Color tCol) {
    final pageItems = _getScheduleForDate(pageDate);

    String dateKey = "${pageDate.year}-${pageDate.month}-${pageDate.day}";
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> externalEvents = _externalEventsCache[dateKey] ?? [];
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> allItems = List.from(pageItems)..addAll(externalEvents);
    allItems.sort((a, b) => (a['time'] as DateTime).compareTo(b['time'] as DateTime));

    final daysEn = ['MON', 'TUE', 'WED', 'THU', 'FRI', 'SAT', 'SUN'];
    String dayStr = daysEn[pageDate.weekday - 1];
    String dateAndDay = "${pageDate.month}/${pageDate.day} $dayStr";

    String? relativeLabel;
    if (index == 10000) relativeLabel = "TODAY";
    else if (index == 9999) relativeLabel = "YESTERDAY";
    else if (index == 10001) relativeLabel = "TOMORROW";

    return Align(
      alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 24, right: 24, top: topPadding + 48.0, bottom: 8.0),
        child: GestureDetector(
          behavior: HitTestBehavior.translucent,
          onVerticalDragUpdate: (details) {
            if (_tasksScrollController.hasClients) {
              double maxScroll = _tasksScrollController.position.maxScrollExtent;
              if (maxScroll > 0) {
                double newOffset = (_tasksScrollController.offset - details.primaryDelta!).clamp(0.0, maxScroll);
                _tasksScrollController.jumpTo(newOffset);
              }
            }
          },
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                SizedBox(
                  height: 20,
                  child: Stack(
                    clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                    children: [
                      if (relativeLabel != null)
                        Positioned(
                          top: -12,
                          left: 0,
                          child: Text(relativeLabel, style: TextStyle(color: tCol.withOpacity(0.5), fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, letterSpacing: 1.0)),
                        ),
                      Align(
                        alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                        child: Text(dateAndDay, style: TextStyle(color: tCol.withOpacity(0.7), fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, height: 1.0)),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                if (allItems.isNotEmpty)
                  Column(
                    children: allItems.map((item) {
                      bool isTask = item['type'] == 'task';
                      bool isExternal = item['type'] == 'external_event';

                      DateTime time = item['time'];
                      String startTimeStr = "${time.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${time.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}";

                      // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・夂ｵゆｺ・凾髢薙・譁・ｭ怜・繧堤函謌・
                      String endTimeStr = "";
                      if (item['endTime'] != null) {
                        DateTime endTime = item['endTime'];
                        endTimeStr = "${endTime.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${endTime.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}";
                      } else {
                        // endTime縺後↑縺・ｴ蜷茨ｼ亥商縺・ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ縺ｪ縺ｩ・峨・1譎る俣蠕後ｒ莉ｮ鄂ｮ
                        DateTime fakeEnd = time.add(const Duration(hours: 1));
                        endTimeStr = "${fakeEnd.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${fakeEnd.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}";
                      }

                      if (isExternal && item['isAllDay'] == true) {
                        startTimeStr = "ALL";
                        endTimeStr = "DAY"; // 邨よ律縺ｮ蝣ｴ蜷医・縲窟LL DAY縲阪・繧医≧縺ｫ隕九○繧・
                      }

                      int status = item['status'] ?? 0;
                      double itemOpacity = 1.0;
                      Color subTextColor = tCol.withOpacity(0.7);
                      String subText = item['subtitle']?.toString() ?? '';

                      if (!isTask && !isExternal) {
                        if (status == 1) { subText = "Ondemand"; itemOpacity = 0.4; }
                        else if (status == 3) { subText = "Exam"; }
                      }

                      return SizedBox(
                          height: 56, // 鬮倥＆縺ｯ蜈・・縺ｾ縺ｾ邯ｭ謖・
                          child: Opacity(
                            opacity: itemOpacity,
                            child: Padding(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 16.0),
                                child: GestureDetector(
                                  behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                                  onTap: () {
                                    if (isExternal) return;
                                    if (isTask) {
                                      if (item['courseId'] == 'custom_t') {
                                        _showCompleteCustomTaskDialog(item['taskId']);
                                      } else {
                                        String url = item['targetUrl'] ?? '';
                                        if (url.isNotEmpty) openSmartUrl(context, url, widget.cookie, title: item['title']);
                                        else {
                                          int cIdx = _courseIndexMap[item['courseId']] ?? -1;
                                          if (cIdx != -1) _navigateToDetail(cIdx, initialBottomIndex: 2, targetTaskId: item['taskId']);
                                        }
                                      }
                                    } else if (item['isCustom'] == true) {
                                      _navigateToCustomDetail(item['courseObj']);
                                    } else {
                                      int cIdx = _courseIndexMap[item['courseId']] ?? -1;
                                      if (cIdx != -1) _navigateToDetail(cIdx, initialBottomIndex: 1);
                                    }
                                  },
                                  child: Row(
                                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                      children: [
                                        // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壽凾髢楢｡ｨ遉ｺ繧ｨ繝ｪ繧｢繧辰olumn縺ｫ縺励※縲・幕蟋九→邨ゆｺ・ｒ邵ｦ縺ｫ荳ｦ縺ｹ繧・
                                        SizedBox(
                                            width: 45,
                                            child: Column(
                                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                              children: [
                                                Text(startTimeStr, style: TextStyle(color: tCol, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, height: 1.0)),
                                                const SizedBox(height: 2), // 蟆代＠髫咎俣繧堤ｩｺ縺代ｋ
                                                Text(endTimeStr, style: TextStyle(color: tCol.withOpacity(0.5), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w400, height: 1.0)), // 蟆上＆縺剰埋縺剰｡ｨ遉ｺ
                                              ],
                                            )
                                        ),
                                        const SizedBox(width: 12),
                                        Expanded(
                                          child: Column(
                                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                            children: [
                                              Row(
                                                children: [
                                                  if (isTask) ...[Icon(Icons.assignment_late, size: 12, color: tCol), const SizedBox(width: 4)],
                                                  if (isExternal) ...[Icon(Icons.event, size: 12, color: tCol.withOpacity(0.5)), const SizedBox(width: 4)],
                                                  Expanded(child: Text(item['title'], style: TextStyle(color: isExternal ? tCol.withOpacity(0.8) : tCol, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: isExternal ? FontWeight.w400 : FontWeight.w300, height: 1.0), maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
                                                ],
                                              ),
                                              const SizedBox(height: 2),
                                              Text(subText, style: TextStyle(color: subTextColor, fontSize: 10, height: 1.0), maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                                            ],
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ]
                                  ),
                                )
                            ),
                          )
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildWeekPage(DateTime startOfWeek, double topPadding, Color tCol) {
    final daysEn = ['MON', 'TUE', 'WED', 'THU', 'FRI', 'SAT', 'SUN'];
    final monthsEn = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
    String monthLabel = monthsEn[startOfWeek.month - 1];

    int minHour = 24;
    int maxHour = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      DateTime d = startOfWeek.add(Duration(days: i));
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> appEvents = _getScheduleForDate(d).where((item) => item['status'] != 2).toList();
      String dateKey = "${d.year}-${d.month}-${d.day}";
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> ext = _externalEventsCache[dateKey] ?? [];
      for (var item in [...appEvents, ...ext]) {
        if (item['isAllDay'] == true) continue;
        DateTime st = item['time'];
        DateTime et = item['endTime'] ?? st.add(const Duration(hours: 1));
        if (st.hour < minHour) minHour = st.hour;
        if (et.hour > maxHour) maxHour = et.hour + (et.minute > 0 ? 1 : 0);
      }
    }

    if (minHour > maxHour) { minHour = 9; maxHour = 18; }
    else { minHour = math.max(0, minHour - 1); maxHour = math.min(24, maxHour + 1); }

    final double pixelsPerMinute = 0.5;
    final double timelineHeight = (maxHour - minHour) * 60 * pixelsPerMinute;

    return Align(
      alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 16, right: 16, top: topPadding + 48.0, bottom: 8.0),
        // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・咼繝｢繝ｼ繝牙酔讒倥↓縲∫ｸｦ繧ｹ繝ｯ繧､繝励ｒ繧ｿ繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繧ｹ繝医・繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｭ繝ｼ繝ｫ縺ｫ豬√＠霎ｼ繧
        child: GestureDetector(
          behavior: HitTestBehavior.translucent,
          onVerticalDragUpdate: (details) {
            if (_tasksScrollController.hasClients) {
              double maxScroll = _tasksScrollController.position.maxScrollExtent;
              if (maxScroll > 0) {
                double newOffset = (_tasksScrollController.offset - details.primaryDelta!).clamp(0.0, maxScroll);
                _tasksScrollController.jumpTo(newOffset);
              }
            }
          },
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6.0),
                  child: SizedBox(
                    height: 28,
                    child: Row(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.baseline,
                      textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                      children: [
                        Text(monthLabel, style: TextStyle(color: tCol.withOpacity(0.9), fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.w200, letterSpacing: 1.2)),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Text("${minHour}:00 - ${maxHour}:00", style: TextStyle(color: tCol.withOpacity(0.4), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w400)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: List.generate(7, (i) {
                    DateTime currentDay = startOfWeek.add(Duration(days: i));
                    bool isToday = currentDay.year == DateTime.now().year && currentDay.month == DateTime.now().month && currentDay.day == DateTime.now().day;

                    List<Map<String, dynamic>> appEvents = _getScheduleForDate(currentDay).where((item) => item['status'] != 2).toList();
                    String dateKey = "${currentDay.year}-${currentDay.month}-${currentDay.day}";
                    List<Map<String, dynamic>> externalEvents = _externalEventsCache[dateKey] ?? [];

                    List<Map<String, dynamic>> dailyEvents = [...appEvents, ...externalEvents];
                    dailyEvents.sort((a, b) => (a['time'] as DateTime).compareTo(b['time'] as DateTime));

                    List<Widget> eventWidgets = [];

                    for (var event in dailyEvents) {
                      bool isExternal = event['type'] == 'external_event';
                      bool isTask = event['type'] == 'task';
                      DateTime startTime = event['time'];
                      DateTime endTime = event['endTime'] ?? startTime.add(const Duration(hours: 1));

                      double topOffset;
                      double height;

                      if (isExternal && event['isAllDay'] == true) {
                        topOffset = 0.0;
                        height = 12.0;
                      } else {
                        int startMinTotal = startTime.hour * 60 + startTime.minute;
                        int endMinTotal = endTime.hour * 60 + endTime.minute;

                        if (startMinTotal < minHour * 60) startMinTotal = minHour * 60;
                        if (endMinTotal > maxHour * 60) endMinTotal = maxHour * 60;

                        int durationMin = endMinTotal - startMinTotal;
                        if (durationMin < 20) durationMin = 20;

                        topOffset = (startMinTotal - minHour * 60) * pixelsPerMinute;
                        height = durationMin * pixelsPerMinute;
                      }

                      Color bgColor = !isExternal ? _getCourseColor(event['courseObj']) : tCol.withOpacity(0.15);
                      Color txtColor = !isExternal ? AppTheme.getTextColorForBackground(bgColor) : tCol;

                      if (isTask) {
                        bgColor = bgColor.withOpacity(0.8);
                      } else if (!isExternal && event['status'] == 1) {
                        bgColor = bgColor.withOpacity(0.3);
                        txtColor = txtColor.withOpacity(0.5);
                      }

                      String title = event['title'] ?? '';

                      eventWidgets.add(
                          Positioned(
                            top: topOffset,
                            left: 0,
                            right: 0,
                            height: height,
                            child: GestureDetector(
                              onTap: () {
                                if (isTask) {
                                  if (event['courseId'] == 'custom_t') _showCompleteCustomTaskDialog(event['taskId']);
                                  else {
                                    String url = event['targetUrl'] ?? '';
                                    if (url.isNotEmpty) openSmartUrl(context, url, widget.cookie, title: event['title']);
                                    else {
                                      int cIdx = _courseIndexMap[event['courseId']] ?? -1;
                                      if (cIdx != -1) _navigateToDetail(cIdx, initialBottomIndex: 2, targetTaskId: event['taskId']);
                                    }
                                  }
                                } else if (!isExternal) {
                                  if (event['isCustom'] == true) _navigateToCustomDetail(event['courseObj']);
                                  else {
                                    int cIdx = _courseIndexMap[event['courseId']] ?? -1;
                                    if (cIdx != -1) _navigateToDetail(cIdx, initialBottomIndex: 1);
                                  }
                                }
                              },
                              child: Container(
                                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 1.0, right: 1.0),
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2.0, vertical: 2.0),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: bgColor,
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                                  border: Border.all(color: AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(isTask ? 0.8 : 0.4), width: isTask ? 1.0 : 0.5),
                                ),
                                child: Text(
                                  title,
                                  style: TextStyle(color: txtColor, fontSize: 7, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, height: 1.1),
                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                  maxLines: math.max(1, (height / 10).floor()),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          )
                      );
                    }

                    return Expanded(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2.0),
                        child: Column(
                          children: [
                            GestureDetector(
                              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                              onTap: () => _switchToDayMode(currentDay),
                              child: Padding(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4.0),
                                child: Column(
                                  children: [
                                    Text(daysEn[i][0], style: TextStyle(color: tCol.withOpacity(isToday ? 1.0 : 0.5), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: isToday ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.w300)),
                                    Text("${currentDay.day}", style: TextStyle(color: tCol.withOpacity(isToday ? 1.0 : 0.5), fontSize: 14, fontWeight: isToday ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.w300)),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                            SizedBox(
                                height: timelineHeight,
                                width: double.infinity,
                                child: Stack(
                                  clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                                  children: eventWidgets,
                                )
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  }),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMonthPage(DateTime pageDate, double topPadding, Color tCol) {
    DateTime firstDayOfMonth = DateTime(pageDate.year, pageDate.month, 1);
    DateTime startDate = firstDayOfMonth.subtract(Duration(days: firstDayOfMonth.weekday - 1));
    final monthsEn = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
    String monthLabel = monthsEn[pageDate.month - 1];

    int daysInMonth = DateTime(pageDate.year, pageDate.month + 1, 0).day;
    int numWeeks = ((firstDayOfMonth.weekday - 1 + daysInMonth) / 7.0).ceil();

    return Align(
      alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 16, right: 16, top: topPadding + 48.0, bottom: 8.0),
        // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・咼繝｢繝ｼ繝牙酔讒倥↓縲∫ｸｦ繧ｹ繝ｯ繧､繝励ｒ繧ｿ繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｪ繧ｹ繝医・繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｭ繝ｼ繝ｫ縺ｫ豬√＠霎ｼ繧
        child: GestureDetector(
          behavior: HitTestBehavior.translucent,
          onVerticalDragUpdate: (details) {
            if (_tasksScrollController.hasClients) {
              double maxScroll = _tasksScrollController.position.maxScrollExtent;
              if (maxScroll > 0) {
                double newOffset = (_tasksScrollController.offset - details.primaryDelta!).clamp(0.0, maxScroll);
                _tasksScrollController.jumpTo(newOffset);
              }
            }
          },
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8.0),
                  child: SizedBox(
                    height: 28,
                    child: Text(monthLabel, style: TextStyle(color: tCol.withOpacity(0.9), fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.w200, letterSpacing: 1.2)),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                  children: ['M', 'T', 'W', 'T', 'F', 'S', 'S'].map((d) =>
                      Expanded(child: Center(child: Text(d, style: TextStyle(color: tCol.withOpacity(0.5), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.w300))))
                  ).toList(),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),

                ...List.generate(numWeeks, (weekIdx) {
                  return Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6.0),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: List.generate(7, (dayIdx) {
                        DateTime currentDay = startDate.add(Duration(days: weekIdx * 7 + dayIdx));
                        bool isCurrentMonth = currentDay.month == pageDate.month;
                        bool isToday = currentDay.year == DateTime.now().year && currentDay.month == DateTime.now().month && currentDay.day == DateTime.now().day;

                        List<Map<String, dynamic>> appEvents = _getScheduleForDate(currentDay).where((item) => item['status'] != 2).toList();
                        String dateKey = "${currentDay.year}-${currentDay.month}-${currentDay.day}";
                        List<Map<String, dynamic>> externalEvents = _externalEventsCache[dateKey] ?? [];

                        return Expanded(
                          child: Container(
                            margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2),
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                              children: [
                                GestureDetector(
                                  behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                                  onTap: () => _switchToDayMode(currentDay),
                                  child: Padding(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4.0, horizontal: 8.0),
                                    child: Text(
                                        "${currentDay.day}",
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                            color: tCol.withOpacity(isToday ? 1.0 : (isCurrentMonth ? 0.7 : 0.2)),
                                            fontSize: 10,
                                            fontWeight: isToday ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.w300
                                        )
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(height: 2),

                                ...appEvents.map((c) {
                                  bool isTask = c['type'] == 'task';
                                  Color cColor = _getCourseColor(c['courseObj']);
                                  bool isOndemand = c['status'] == 1;

                                  return GestureDetector(
                                    onTap: () {
                                      if (isTask) {
                                        if (c['courseId'] == 'custom_t') {
                                          _showCompleteCustomTaskDialog(c['taskId']);
                                        } else {
                                          String url = c['targetUrl'] ?? '';
                                          if (url.isNotEmpty) openSmartUrl(context, url, widget.cookie, title: c['title']);
                                          else {
                                            int cIdx = _courseIndexMap[c['courseId']] ?? -1;
                                            if (cIdx != -1) _navigateToDetail(cIdx, initialBottomIndex: 2, targetTaskId: c['taskId']);
                                          }
                                        }
                                      } else {
                                        if (c['isCustom'] == true) _navigateToCustomDetail(c['courseObj']);
                                        else {
                                          int cIdx = _courseIndexMap[c['courseId']] ?? -1;
                                          if (cIdx != -1) _navigateToDetail(cIdx, initialBottomIndex: 1);
                                        }
                                      }
                                    },
                                    child: Container(
                                      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 2),
                                      height: isTask ? 6 : 10,
                                      width: double.infinity,
                                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                        color: isTask ? cColor.withOpacity(0.9) : cColor.withOpacity(isOndemand ? 0.3 : 0.8),
                                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(isTask ? 3 : 5),
                                        border: isTask ? Border.all(color: tCol.withOpacity(0.4), width: 0.5) : null,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  );
                                }).toList(),

                                ...externalEvents.take(3).map((e) {
                                  return Container(
                                    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 2),
                                    height: 3,
                                    width: double.infinity,
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: tCol.withOpacity(0.3),
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(1.5),
                                    ),
                                  );
                                }).toList(),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      }),
                    ),
                  );
                }),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInnerCalendar(double topPadding, bool isDark, double t, double baseItemWidth, int maxPeriod, double baseSpacing) {
    double currentItemHeight = baseItemWidth - t * (baseItemWidth - 12.0);
    double currentSpacing = baseSpacing - t * (baseSpacing - 1.0);
    double textOpacity = math.max(0.0, 1.0 - t * 3.0);




    return Container(
        key: const ValueKey('calendar'),
        width: double.infinity,
        padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: topPadding + 8.0, bottom: 12.0, left: 8.0, right: 8.0),
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                if (textOpacity > 0)
                  Opacity(
                    opacity: textOpacity,
                    child: SizedBox(
                      height: 24.0 * textOpacity,
                      child: Row(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                          children: ['MON','TUE','WED','THU','FRI','SAT'].asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                            int idx = entry.key; bool isToday = (DateTime.now().weekday - 1) == idx;
                            Color capsuleColor = isDark ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.08) : Colors.black.withOpacity(0.15);
                            return Container(
                              width: baseItemWidth,
                              // 笘・_isCapturing・医せ繧ｯ繧ｷ繝ｧ荳ｭ・峨↑繧峨き繝励そ繝ｫ繧呈緒逕ｻ縺励↑縺・
                              decoration: (isToday && !_isCapturing) ? BoxDecoration(color: capsuleColor, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)) : null,
                              alignment: Alignment.center,
                              child: Text(entry.value, style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                            );
                          }).toList()
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                if (textOpacity > 0)
                  SizedBox(height: 4.0 * textOpacity),
                ...List.generate(maxPeriod + 1, (periodIndex) {
                  return Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: periodIndex == maxPeriod ? 0 : currentSpacing),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: List.generate(6, (dayIndex) {

                        // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壼酔縺俶屆譌･繝ｻ譎る剞縺ｮ縺吶∋縺ｦ縺ｮ謗域･ｭ繧貞叙蠕・
                        final matches = _weeklySchedule.where((s) => s['day'] == dayIndex && s['period'] == periodIndex).toList();
                        if (matches.isEmpty) return SizedBox(width: baseItemWidth, height: currentItemHeight);

                        // 笘・繧ｻ繝ｫ謠冗判逕ｨ繝倥Ν繝代・髢｢謨ｰ・亥・逕ｻ髱｢逕ｨ繝ｻ蜊雁・逕ｨ縺ｮ蜈ｱ騾壼・逅・ｼ・
                        Widget buildCell(Map<String, dynamic> match, double height, bool isHalf) {
                          Color color = _getCourseColor(match['course']);
                          return GestureDetector(
                            behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                            onTap: () {
                              if (match['course']['is_custom'] == true) {
                                _navigateToCustomDetail(match['course']);
                              } else {
                                int courseIdx = _courseIndexMap[match['course']['id']] ?? -1;
                                if (courseIdx != -1) _navigateToDetail(courseIdx);
                              }
                            },
                            child: Container(
                              width: baseItemWidth,
                              height: height,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(t > 0.8 ? 6 : (isHalf ? 6 : 12))),
                              alignment: Alignment.center,
                              padding: EdgeInsets.all(isHalf ? 1.0 : 2.0),
                              child: Opacity(
                                opacity: textOpacity,
                                child: Text(CourseTitleParser.getCleanTitle(match['course']['name']), style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.getTextColorForBackground(color), fontSize: isHalf ? 7.5 : 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.w300, height: 1.1), textAlign: TextAlign.center, maxLines: isHalf ? 2 : 4, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        }

                        // 笘・Q驕ｸ謚樔ｸｭ縺ｪ縺ｩ縲・縺､縺励°陦ｨ遉ｺ蟇ｾ雎｡縺後↑縺・ｴ蜷医・騾壼ｸｸ縺ｮ豁｣譁ｹ蠖｢繝槭せ
                        if (matches.length == 1) {
                          return buildCell(matches.first, currentItemHeight, false);
                        }

                        // 笘・蟄ｦ譛滄∈謚樔ｸｭ縺ｪ縺ｩ縲・縺､莉･荳翫・謗域･ｭ縺碁㍾縺ｪ縺｣縺ｦ縺・ｋ蝣ｴ蜷医・繝槭せ繧貞濠蛻・↓蛻・牡縺励※荳贋ｸ九↓驟咲ｽｮ
                        Map<String, dynamic>? firstHalfMatch;
                        Map<String, dynamic>? secondHalfMatch;

                        for (var m in matches) {
                          String rawName = m['course']['raw_name'] ?? m['course']['name'] ?? '';
                          String termName = m['course']['term']?['name'] ?? '';
                          String combined = "$rawName $termName".toUpperCase();

                          // Q2, Q4, 蠕梧悄, 蠕悟濠繧峨＠縺阪く繝ｼ繝ｯ繝ｼ繝峨ｒ謖√▽繧ゅ・縺ｯ荳区ｮｵ縺ｸ縲ゅ◎繧御ｻ･螟悶・荳頑ｮｵ縺ｸ縲・
                          bool isQ2 = combined.contains("Q2") || combined.contains("2Q") || combined.contains("隨ｬ2繧ｯ繧ｩ繝ｼ繧ｿ繝ｼ") || combined.contains("譏･蟄ｦ譛溷ｾ悟濠");
                          bool isQ4 = combined.contains("Q4") || combined.contains("4Q") || combined.contains("隨ｬ4繧ｯ繧ｩ繝ｼ繧ｿ繝ｼ") || combined.contains("遘句ｭｦ譛溷ｾ悟濠");
                          bool isLate = combined.contains("蠕悟濠") || combined.contains("蠕梧悄");

                          if (isQ2 || isQ4 || isLate) {
                            secondHalfMatch ??= m;
                          } else {
                            firstHalfMatch ??= m;
                          }
                        }

                        // 繝代・繧ｹ縺ｫ螟ｱ謨励＠縺ｦ荳｡譁ｹ縺ｨ繧ゆｸ頑ｮｵ(縺ｾ縺溘・荳区ｮｵ)縺ｫ蛛上▲縺溷ｴ蜷医・繝輔か繝ｼ繝ｫ繝舌ャ繧ｯ・亥ｼｷ蛻ｶ逧・↓1縺､逶ｮ縺ｨ2縺､逶ｮ繧貞・縺代ｋ・・
                        if (firstHalfMatch == null) firstHalfMatch = matches[0];
                        if (secondHalfMatch == null) secondHalfMatch = matches[1];
                        if (firstHalfMatch == secondHalfMatch) secondHalfMatch = matches[1];

                        double halfHeight = (currentItemHeight - 2.0) / 2.0; // 髫咎俣繧・px遨ｺ縺代ｋ
                        return SizedBox(
                            width: baseItemWidth,
                            height: currentItemHeight,
                            child: Column(
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                              children: [
                                buildCell(firstHalfMatch, halfHeight, true),  // 荳頑ｮｵ・・1/Q3遲会ｼ・
                                buildCell(secondHalfMatch, halfHeight, true), // 荳区ｮｵ・・2/Q4遲会ｼ・
                              ],
                            )
                        );
                      }),
                    ),
                  );
                }),
              ]
          ),
        )
    );
  }




  // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壹ヰ繧ｰ縺ｮ蜴溷屏縺ｨ縺ｪ縺｣縺ｦ縺・◆閾ｪ蜑阪・繧ｹ繝ｯ繧､繝怜愛螳壹ｒ蜑企勁縺励√ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ繝輔ぅ繝ｫ繧ｿ繝ｪ繝ｳ繧ｰ繧貞崋螳・
  // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・夲ｼ九・繧ｿ繝ｳ縺ｮ蟶ｸ譎り｡ｨ遉ｺ縺ｨ縲√せ繝ｯ繧､繝礼黄逅・音諤ｧ縺ｮ譛驕ｩ蛹・
  Widget _buildFullTasksContent() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final nextWeek = now.add(const Duration(days: 7));


    // 蟾ｦ蛛ｴ・啀ENDING逕ｨ繝・・繧ｿ
    final overdueTasks = _filterTasks((due) => due.isBefore(now), wantCompleted: false);
    final next7DaysTasks = _filterTasks((due) => due.isAfter(now) && due.isBefore(nextWeek), wantCompleted: false);
    final futureTasks = _filterTasks((due) => due.isAfter(nextWeek) || due.isAtSameMomentAs(nextWeek), wantCompleted: false);


    // 蜿ｳ蛛ｴ・咾OMPLETED逕ｨ繝・・繧ｿ
    List<dynamic> allCompletedTasks = _filterTasks((due) => true, wantCompleted: true);
    allCompletedTasks.sort((a, b) {
      DateTime timeA = DateTime.tryParse(a['submission']?['submitted_at'] ?? a['due_at'] ?? '') ?? DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);
      DateTime timeB = DateTime.tryParse(b['submission']?['submitted_at'] ?? b['due_at'] ?? '') ?? DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);
      return timeB.compareTo(timeA);
    });


    double topPadding = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 24.0;
    bool isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;


    return Column(
      children: [
        Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: topPadding, left: 16.0, right: 16.0, bottom: 8.0),
            child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  GestureDetector(
                    behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                    onTap: _handleBackNavigation,
                    child: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Icon(Icons.arrow_back_ios, color: AppTheme.textMain(context), size: 20),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          Text("TASKS", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, letterSpacing: 1.0)),
                        ]
                    ),
                  ),
                  // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壽擅莉ｶ蛻・ｲ・(if) 繧貞炎髯､縺励∝ｸｸ縺ｫ陦ｨ遉ｺ縺輔ｌ繧九ｈ縺・↓縺励∪縺励◆
                  GestureDetector(
                    behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                    onTap: _showAddTaskDialog,
                    child: Icon(Icons.add, color: isDark ? Colors.white : Colors.black, size: 28),
                  ),
                ]
            )
        ),


        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8.0, vertical: 8.0),
          child: Container(
            height: 36,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppTheme.panel(context),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
            ),
            child: AnimatedBuilder(
                animation: _taskPageController,
                builder: (context, child) {
                  double page = 0.0;
                  if (_taskPageController.hasClients && _taskPageController.position.haveDimensions) {
                    page = _taskPageController.page ?? (_showCompletedTasks ? 1.0 : 0.0);
                  } else {
                    page = _showCompletedTasks ? 1.0 : 0.0;
                  }
                  return Stack(
                    children: [
                      Align(
                        alignment: Alignment(page * 2 - 1, 0),
                        child: FractionallySizedBox(
                          widthFactor: 0.5,
                          heightFactor: 1.0,
                          child: Container(
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: AppTheme.textMain(context).withOpacity(0.1),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: GestureDetector(
                              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                              onTap: () {
                                _taskPageController.animateToPage(0, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300), curve: Curves.easeOutCubic);
                              },
                              child: Center(child: Text("PENDING", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                            ),
                          ),
                          Expanded(
                            child: GestureDetector(
                              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                              onTap: () {
                                _taskPageController.animateToPage(1, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300), curve: Curves.easeOutCubic);
                              },
                              child: Center(child: Text("COMPLETED", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  );
                }
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Expanded(
            child: PageView(
                controller: _taskPageController,
                // 笘・繧ｹ繝ｯ繧､繝玲─繧偵ｈ繧願・辟ｶ縺ｫ縺吶ｋ縺溘ａ縺ｮ迚ｩ逅・音諤ｧ
                physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(parent: AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics()),
                onPageChanged: (i) => setState(() => _showCompletedTasks = i == 1),
                children: [
                  // 蟾ｦ蛛ｴ・壽悴謠仙・繝ｪ繧ｹ繝・
                  ListView(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0, right: 8.0, bottom: 120.0),
                    physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                    children: [
                      if (next7DaysTasks.isNotEmpty) ...[
                        Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0, bottom: 8.0), child: Text("NEXT 7 DAYS", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textSub(context), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                        _buildGroupedTasks(next7DaysTasks, isCompletedList: false),
                        const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      ],
                      if (overdueTasks.isNotEmpty) ...[
                        Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0, bottom: 8.0), child: Text("OVERDUE", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textSub(context), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                        _buildGroupedTasks(overdueTasks, isOverdue: true, isCompletedList: false),
                        const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      ],
                      if (futureTasks.isNotEmpty) ...[
                        Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0, bottom: 8.0), child: Text("FUTURE TASKS", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textSub(context), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                        _buildGroupedTasks(futureTasks, isCompletedList: false),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                  // 蜿ｳ蛛ｴ・壼ｮ御ｺ・ｸ医∩繝ｪ繧ｹ繝・
                  ListView(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0, right: 8.0, bottom: 120.0),
                    physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
                    children: [
                      if (allCompletedTasks.isNotEmpty)
                        _buildGroupedTasks(allCompletedTasks, isCompletedList: true),
                    ],
                  )
                ]
            )
        )
      ],
    );
  }




  Widget _buildFullMessagesContent() {
    final unreadMessages = _filterMessages((m) => !_dismissedMessageIds.contains(m['id'].toString()) && (m['read_state'] == null || m['read_state'] != 'read'));
    final readMessages = _filterMessages((m) => _dismissedMessageIds.contains(m['id'].toString()) || (m['read_state'] != null && m['read_state'] == 'read'));


    double topPadding = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 24.0;


    return Column(
      children: [
        Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: topPadding, left: 16.0, right: 16.0, bottom: 16.0),
            child: Row(
                children: [
                  GestureDetector(
                      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                      onTap: _handleBackNavigation,
                      child: Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Icon(Icons.arrow_back_ios, color: AppTheme.textMain(context), size: 20),
                            const SizedBox(width: 8),
                            Text("ALL MESSAGES", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, letterSpacing: 1.0)),
                          ]
                      )
                  ),
                ]
            )
        ),
        Expanded(
            child: ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0, right: 8.0, bottom: 120.0),
              physics: const ClampingScrollPhysics(),
              children: [
                if (unreadMessages.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0, bottom: 8.0), child: Text("UNREAD", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textSub(context), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                  _buildGroupedMessages(unreadMessages, isUnread: true),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                ],
                if (readMessages.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0, bottom: 8.0), child: Text("READ", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textSub(context), fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                  Opacity(
                      opacity: 0.5,
                      child: _buildGroupedMessages(readMessages, isUnread: false)
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            )
        )
      ],
    );
  }




  Widget _buildBottomNavigation() {
    bool isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;


    int leftFlex = (80 - _dragPosition * 60).toInt();
    int rightFlex = (20 + _dragPosition * 60).toInt();


    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(8.0, 0, 8.0, MediaQuery.of(context).padding.bottom + 16.0),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          AnimatedBuilder(
            // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・喟spinAnimController繧ら屮隕門ｯｾ雎｡縺ｫ霑ｽ蜉
              animation: Listenable.merge([_colorAnimController, _errorAnimController, _spinAnimController]),
              builder: (context, child) {
                Color bg;
                IconData iconData;


                bool isLoading = _isLoadingAssignments || _isLoadingMessages;


                // 濶ｲ縺ｮ螳夂ｾｩ
                Color defaultPanelColor = AppTheme.panel(context);
                Color defaultTextColor = AppTheme.textMain(context); // 繝ｩ繧､繝域凾縺ｯ鮟偵√ム繝ｼ繧ｯ譎ゅ・逋ｽ
                Color appBgColor = AppTheme.bg(context);


                if (!widget.isAuthenticated || _hasApiError) {
                  // 繝ｭ繧ｰ繧｢繧ｦ繝育憾諷・or 繝医Λ繝悶Ν譎ゑｼ夊ｵ､ 竍・閭梧勹濶ｲ 縺ｧ貊代ｉ縺九↓轤ｹ貊・
                  bg = Color.lerp(AppTheme.accent, appBgColor, _errorAnimController.value) ?? AppTheme.accent;
                  iconData = !widget.isAuthenticated ? Icons.login : Icons.sync;
                } else if (isLoading) {
                  // 隱ｭ縺ｿ霎ｼ縺ｿ譎ゑｼ夐ｻ・竍・逋ｽ 縺ｧ轤ｹ貊・
                  bg = Color.lerp(Colors.black, Colors.white, _colorAnimController.value) ?? Colors.black;
                  iconData = Icons.sync;
                } else {
                  // 豁｣蟶ｸ譎ゑｼ夊ｨｭ螳壹・繧ｿ繝ｳ縺ｪ縺ｩ縺ｨ蜷後§濶ｲ縺ｧ蝗ｺ螳・
                  bg = defaultPanelColor;
                  iconData = Icons.sync;
                }


                // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壹い繧､繧ｳ繝ｳ縺ｮ濶ｲ縺ｯ蟶ｸ縺ｫ繝・ヵ繧ｩ繝ｫ繝茨ｼ医Λ繧､繝医〒鮟偵√ム繝ｼ繧ｯ縺ｧ逋ｽ・峨↓蝗ｺ螳・
                Widget iconWidget = Icon(iconData, color: defaultTextColor);


                // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・夊ｪｭ縺ｿ霎ｼ縺ｿ荳ｭ縺ｮ縺ｿ繧｢繧､繧ｳ繝ｳ繧貞屓霆｢縺輔○繧・
                if (isLoading) {
                  iconWidget = Transform.rotate(
                    angle: _spinAnimController.value * 2.0 * math.pi, // 0縺九ｉ2ﾏ・・60蠎ｦ・峨∈蝗櫁ｻ｢
                    child: iconWidget,
                  );
                }


                return GestureDetector(
                  onTap: () {
                    if (!widget.isAuthenticated) {
                      widget.onLogin();
                    } else if (!isLoading) { // 隱ｭ縺ｿ霎ｼ縺ｿ荳ｭ縺ｯ騾｣謇薙ｒ髦ｲ豁｢
                      _fetchDataParallel();
                    }
                  },
                  child: Container(
                    width: 60, height: 60,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(color: bg, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                    child: iconWidget, // 笘・蝗櫁ｻ｢蜃ｦ逅・ｒ騾壹＠縺溘え繧｣繧ｸ繧ｧ繝・ヨ繧定｡ｨ遉ｺ
                  ),
                );
              }
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),


          Expanded(
            child: LayoutBuilder(
                builder: (context, constraints) {
                  final double toggleWidth = constraints.maxWidth;
                  return GestureDetector(
                    onHorizontalDragUpdate: (details) {
                      setState(() {
                        _dragPosition += details.delta.dx / (toggleWidth * 0.4);
                        _dragPosition = _dragPosition.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                      });
                    },
                    onHorizontalDragEnd: (details) {
                      // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・喟dragPosition繧堤峩謗･荳頑嶌縺阪○縺壹∫岼讓吩ｽ咲ｽｮ(targetPosition)縺ｸ迴ｾ蝨ｨ蝨ｰ縺九ｉ繧｢繝九Γ繝ｼ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ縺輔○繧・
                      double targetPosition;
                      if (details.primaryVelocity != null && details.primaryVelocity!.abs() > 300) {
                        targetPosition = details.primaryVelocity! > 0 ? 0.0 : 1.0;
                      } else {
                        targetPosition = _dragPosition > 0.5 ? 1.0 : 0.0;
                      }


                      _dragAnimController.value = _dragPosition;
                      _dragAnimController.animateTo(targetPosition, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 150), curve: Curves.easeOutCubic);
                    },
                    child: Container(
                      height: 60,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppTheme.panel(context), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30)),
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4.0),
                      child: Stack(
                        children: [
                          AnimatedAlign(
                            duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 150),
                            curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                            alignment: Alignment(_dragPosition * 2 - 1, 0),
                            child: FractionallySizedBox(
                              widthFactor: 0.8,
                              heightFactor: 1.0,
                              child: Container(
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: isDark ? Colors.white24 : Colors.black12,
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(26),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                flex: leftFlex,
                                child: GestureDetector(
                                    behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                                    onTap: () {
                                      _dragAnimController.animateTo(0.0, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300), curve: Curves.easeOutCubic);
                                      if (_currentViewState != ViewState.home) _handleBackNavigation();
                                    },
                                    child: Center(
                                        child: Row(
                                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                                            children: [
                                              Icon(Icons.format_list_bulleted, color: AppTheme.textMain(context), size: 20),
                                              if (_dragPosition < 0.5) ...[
                                                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                                Flexible(child: Text("TASKS", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, letterSpacing: 1.2), overflow: TextOverflow.clip))
                                              ]
                                            ]
                                        )
                                    )
                                ),
                              ),
                              Expanded(
                                flex: rightFlex,
                                child: GestureDetector(
                                    behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                                    onTap: () {
                                      _dragAnimController.animateTo(1.0, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300), curve: Curves.easeOutCubic);
                                      if (_currentViewState != ViewState.home) _handleBackNavigation();
                                    },
                                    child: Center(
                                        child: Row(
                                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                                            children: [
                                              Icon(Icons.library_books, color: AppTheme.textMain(context), size: 20),
                                              if (_dragPosition >= 0.5) ...[
                                                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                                Flexible(child: Text("COURSES", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, letterSpacing: 1.2), overflow: TextOverflow.clip))
                                              ]
                                            ]
                                        )
                                    )
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),


          Container(
            width: 60, height: 60,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppTheme.panel(context), shape: BoxShape.circle),
            child: IconButton(
              icon: Icon(Icons.settings, color: AppTheme.textMain(context)),
              onPressed: widget.onSettings,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }




  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return PopScope(
      canPop: _currentViewState == ViewState.home,
      onPopInvoked: (didPop) {
        if (didPop) return;
        if (_currentViewState != ViewState.home) {
          _handleBackNavigation();
        }
      },
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          Positioned.fill(
              child: AnimatedSwitcher(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                child: _currentViewState == ViewState.home
                    ? _buildHomeContent()
                    : _currentViewState == ViewState.fullTasks
                    ? _buildFullTasksContent()
                    : _buildFullMessagesContent(),
              )
          ),




          Positioned(
            bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0,
            // 笘・繧ｰ繝ｩ繝・・繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ縺ｮ霍晞屬繧偵＆繧峨↓莨ｸ縺ｰ縺・(140px + 荳矩Κ菴咏區)
            height: 140.0 + MediaQuery.of(context).padding.bottom + 16.0,
            child: IgnorePointer(
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    height: 140.0,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      gradient: LinearGradient(
                        begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                        end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                        // 笘・遨ｶ讌ｵ縺ｫ貊代ｉ縺九↑11谿ｵ髫弱・繧､繝ｼ繧ｸ繝ｳ繧ｰ繧ｫ繝ｼ繝・
                        colors: [
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.00),
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.02),
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.06),
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.12),
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.22),
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.35),
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.52),
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.72),
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.88),
                          AppTheme.bg(context).withOpacity(0.97),
                          AppTheme.bg(context), // 縺薙％縺ｧ螳悟・縺ｫ陞榊粋
                        ],
                        stops: const [0.0, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.75, 0.88, 0.96, 1.0],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  // 荳矩Κ繝舌・繧医ｊ荳九・菴咏區驛ｨ蛻・・螳悟・縺ｫ閭梧勹濶ｲ縺ｧ蝪励ｊ縺､縺ｶ縺・
                  Expanded(
                    child: Container(
                      color: AppTheme.bg(context),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),




          Positioned(
              bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0,
              child: _buildBottomNavigation()
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


// 笘・霑ｽ蜉・・/2繧ｹ繝ｯ繧､繝励〒豁｢縺ｾ繧翫∝・蠎ｦ繧ｹ繝ｯ繧､繝励〒豸医∴繧九き繧ｹ繧ｿ繝繧ｦ繧｣繧ｸ繧ｧ繝・ヨ
class _SwipeToDeleteWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final VoidCallback onDelete;


  const _SwipeToDeleteWidget({super.key, required this.child, required this.onDelete});


  @override
  State<_SwipeToDeleteWidget> createState() => _SwipeToDeleteWidgetState();
}


class _SwipeToDeleteWidgetState extends State<_SwipeToDeleteWidget> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _controller;
  late Animation<double> _animation;
  double _dragOffset = 0.0;
  bool _isHalfOpen = false;


  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _controller = AnimationController(vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250));
    _animation = Tween<double>(begin: 0, end: 0).animate(_controller);
    _controller.addListener(() {
      setState(() {
        _dragOffset = _animation.value;
      });
    });
  }


  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }


  void _handleDragUpdate(DragUpdateDetails details) {
    setState(() {
      _dragOffset -= details.delta.dx;
      if (_dragOffset < 0) _dragOffset = 0;
    });
  }


  void _handleDragEnd(DragEndDetails details) {
    double maxOffset = MediaQuery.of(context).size.width;
    double halfOffset = maxOffset * 0.5; // 笘・1/2縺ｮ菴咲ｽｮ


    if (!_isHalfOpen) {
      // 譛蛻昴・繧ｹ繝ｯ繧､繝暦ｼ・/2繧定ｶ・∴縺溘°縲∝兇縺・ｈ縺丞ｷｦ縺ｫ繧ｹ繝ｯ繧､繝励＆繧後◆繧牙濠蛻・幕縺・
      if (_dragOffset > halfOffset * 0.5 || (details.primaryVelocity != null && details.primaryVelocity! < -300)) {
        _openHalf(halfOffset);
      } else {
        _close();
      }
    } else {
      // 蜊雁・髢九＞縺ｦ縺・ｋ迥ｶ諷九°繧峨・繧ｹ繝ｯ繧､繝・
      if (_dragOffset > halfOffset + 40.0 || (details.primaryVelocity != null && details.primaryVelocity! < -300)) {
        _delete(maxOffset);
      } else if (_dragOffset < halfOffset - 40.0 || (details.primaryVelocity != null && details.primaryVelocity! > 300)) {
        _close();
      } else {
        _openHalf(halfOffset); // 蟆代＠蜍輔°縺励◆縺縺代↑繧牙濠蛻・・迥ｶ諷九↓謌ｻ繧・
      }
    }
  }


  void _openHalf(double halfOffset) {
    _isHalfOpen = true;
    _animation = Tween<double>(begin: _dragOffset, end: halfOffset).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _controller, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic));
    _controller.forward(from: 0);
  }


  void _close() {
    _isHalfOpen = false;
    _animation = Tween<double>(begin: _dragOffset, end: 0.0).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _controller, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic));
    _controller.forward(from: 0);
  }


  void _delete(double maxOffset) {
    _animation = Tween<double>(begin: _dragOffset, end: maxOffset).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _controller, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic));
    _controller.forward(from: 0).then((_) {
      widget.onDelete();
    });
  }


  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      onHorizontalDragUpdate: _handleDragUpdate,
      onHorizontalDragEnd: _handleDragEnd,
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          Positioned.fill(
            child: Container(
              alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 32),
              // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・夊ｧ剃ｸｸ謖・ｮ壹ｒ蜑企勁縺励∬ｦｪ縺ｮ繧ｦ繧｣繧ｸ繧ｧ繝・ヨ蠖｢迥ｶ・医き繝ｼ繝峨・譫・峨↓萓晏ｭ倥＆縺帙ｋ
              color: const Color(0xFFD0312D),
              child: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, color: Colors.white, size: 28),
            ),
          ),
          Transform.translate(
            offset: Offset(-_dragOffset, 0),
            child: widget.child,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:io'; // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・唹S繧貞愛螳壹☆繧九◆繧√↓蠢・ｦ・
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_inappwebview/flutter_inappwebview.dart';

import '../core/app_theme.dart';
import '../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../utils/lms_adapter.dart';
//login_screen.dart

class OktaLoginScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback onLoginSuccess;
  const OktaLoginScreen({super.key, required this.onLoginSuccess});
  @override State<OktaLoginScreen> createState() => _OktaLoginScreenState();
}


class _OktaLoginScreenState extends State<OktaLoginScreen> {
  bool _isSuccessTriggered = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _clearWebViewCache(); // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・夂判髱｢繧帝幕縺上◆縺ｳ縺ｫ繧ｭ繝｣繝・す繝･縺ｨCookie繧貞､ｧ謗・勁縺吶ｋ
  }

  // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・壼商縺・お繝ｩ繝ｼ迥ｶ諷九ｒ蠑輔″邯吶′縺ｪ縺・◆繧√・螟ｧ謗・勁蜃ｦ逅・
  Future<void> _clearWebViewCache() async {
    try {
      await CookieManager.instance().deleteAllCookies();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Cookie clear error: $e");
    }
  }

  Future<void> _checkLoginStatus(Uri? url) async {
    if (url == null || _isSuccessTriggered) return;
    if (url.host == 'lms.keio.jp') {
      try {
        final cookies = await CookieManager.instance().getCookies(url: WebUri(canvasBaseUrl));
        if (cookies.any((c) => c.name.contains('canvas_session') || c.name == '_csrf_token')) {
          final cookieString = cookies.map((c) => "${c.name}=${c.value}").join("; ");
          final isValid = await AuthService.validateSession(cookieString);
          if (isValid && !_isSuccessTriggered) {
            _isSuccessTriggered = true;
            await AuthService.saveSession(cookieString);
            if (mounted) { Navigator.pop(context); widget.onLoginSuccess(); }
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・唹kta縺ｮ縲係ebView蠑ｾ縺阪阪ｒ螳悟・縺ｫ蝗樣∩縺吶ｋ縺溘ａ縺ｮ蛛ｽ陬・ser-Agent
    final String fakeUserAgent = Platform.isIOS
        ? 'Mozilla/5.0 (iPhone; CPU iPhone OS 17_0 like Mac OS X) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 (KHTML, like Gecko) Version/17.0 Mobile/15E148 Safari/604.1'
        : 'Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 14; Pixel 8) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Mobile Safari/537.36';

    return Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppTheme.bg(context),
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.panel(context),
          elevation: 0,
          leading: IconButton(
              icon: Icon(Icons.close, color: AppTheme.textMain(context)),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context)
          ),
          title: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text("lms.keio.jp login", style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontSize: 14)),
            ],
          ),
          centerTitle: true,
        ),
        body: SafeArea(
            child: InAppWebView(
                initialUrlRequest: URLRequest(url: WebUri(canvasBaseUrl)),
                initialSettings: InAppWebViewSettings(
                    javaScriptEnabled: true,
                    domStorageEnabled: true,
                    thirdPartyCookiesEnabled: true,
                    sharedCookiesEnabled: true,
                    supportMultipleWindows: true,
                    javaScriptCanOpenWindowsAutomatically: true,
                    userAgent: fakeUserAgent, // 笘・縺薙％縺ｧ蛛ｽ陬・ｼ・
                    cacheEnabled: false,      // 笘・繧ｭ繝｣繝・す繝･繧剃ｽｿ繧上↑縺・
                    clearCache: true          // 笘・髢九￥縺ｨ縺阪↓繧ｭ繝｣繝・す繝･繧偵け繝ｪ繧｢
                ),
                onLoadStop: (c, url) => _checkLoginStatus(url),
                onUpdateVisitedHistory: (c, url, isR) => _checkLoginStatus(url)
            )
        )
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_inappwebview/flutter_inappwebview.dart';
import 'package:flutter_cached_pdfview/flutter_cached_pdfview.dart';

import '../core/app_theme.dart';
import '../utils/lms_adapter.dart';
//media_viewers.dart

class InAppBrowserScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String url;
  final String cookie;
  const InAppBrowserScreen({super.key, required this.url, required this.cookie});
  @override
  State<InAppBrowserScreen> createState() => _InAppBrowserScreenState();
}


class _InAppBrowserScreenState extends State<InAppBrowserScreen> {
  bool _isTop = true;
  InAppWebViewController? _webViewController;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    SystemChrome.setEnabledSystemUIMode(SystemUiMode.edgeToEdge);
    // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・壹ヶ繝ｩ繧ｦ繧ｶ繧帝哩縺倥◆繧峨√◎縺ｮ繝悶Λ繧ｦ繧ｶ縺梧ｺ懊ａ縺滉ｸ譎ら噪縺ｪ繧ｭ繝｣繝・す繝･繧貞炎髯､縺吶ｋ
    _webViewController?.clearCache();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final double statusBarHeight = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top;

    return PopScope(
        canPop: false,
        onPopInvoked: (didPop) async {
          if (didPop) return;
          if (_webViewController != null && await _webViewController!.canGoBack()) {
            _webViewController!.goBack();
          } else {
            if (context.mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
          }
        },
        child: Scaffold(
            backgroundColor: AppTheme.bg(context),
            body: Stack(
                children: [
                  AnimatedPositioned(
                      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                      curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                      top: _isTop ? statusBarHeight : 0,
                      bottom: _isTop ? -statusBarHeight : 0,
                      left: 0,
                      right: 0,
                      // 1. InAppBrowserScreen 縺ｮ InAppWebView 繧剃ｻ･荳九・繧医≧縺ｫ菫ｮ豁｣縺励∪縺・
                      child: InAppWebView(
                          onWebViewCreated: (controller) => _webViewController = controller,
                          // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・哂PI縺ｧ菴ｿ縺｣縺ｦ縺・ｋ繧ｯ繝・く繝ｼ繧偵・繝・ム繝ｼ縺ｫ逶ｴ謗･豕ｨ蜈･縺吶ｋ
                          initialUrlRequest: URLRequest(
                            url: WebUri(widget.url),
                            headers: {'Cookie': widget.cookie},
                          ),
                          initialSettings: InAppWebViewSettings(
                            javaScriptEnabled: true,
                            domStorageEnabled: true,
                            // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・壹Ο繧ｰ繧､繝ｳ逕ｻ髱｢縺ｮ繧ｯ繝・く繝ｼ・郁ｪ崎ｨｼ諠・ｱ・峨ｒ蜈ｱ譛峨☆繧句ｿ・郁ｨｭ螳夲ｼ・
                            sharedCookiesEnabled: true,
                            thirdPartyCookiesEnabled: true,
                            useShouldOverrideUrlLoading: true,
                            mediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture: true,
                            allowsInlineMediaPlayback: true,
                          ),
                          onScrollChanged: (c, x, y) {
                            if (y <= 0 && !_isTop) setState(() => _isTop = true);
                            else if (y > 5 && _isTop) setState(() => _isTop = false);
                          },
                          shouldOverrideUrlLoading: (c, nav) async {
                            final uri = nav.request.url;
                            if (uri != null) {
                              final urlStr = uri.toString();
                              final urlLower = urlStr.toLowerCase();
                              bool isVideo = urlLower.contains('isvideo=1') || urlLower.contains('.mp4') || urlLower.contains('.mov') || urlLower.contains('.webm');

                              if (urlStr.contains('/files/') && !urlStr.contains('download') && !isVideo) {
                                final downloadUrl = "${urlStr.split('?').first}/download?download_frd=1";
                                Future.microtask(() => Navigator.pushReplacement(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => PdfViewerScreen(url: downloadUrl, cookie: widget.cookie))));
                                return NavigationActionPolicy.CANCEL;
                              }
                            }
                            return NavigationActionPolicy.ALLOW;
                          },
                          onDownloadStartRequest: (c, req) async {
                            try {
                              if (await canLaunchUrl(req.url)) await launchUrl(req.url, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
                            } catch (e) {}
                          }
                      )
                  )
                ]
            )
        )
    );
  }
}


class PdfViewerScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String url;
  final String cookie;
  const PdfViewerScreen({super.key, required this.url, required this.cookie});
  @override
  State<PdfViewerScreen> createState() => _PdfViewerScreenState();
}


class _PdfViewerScreenState extends State<PdfViewerScreen> {
  bool _isTop = true;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final double statusBarHeight = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top;
    return Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppTheme.bg(context),
        body: Stack(
            children: [
              AnimatedPositioned(
                  duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                  curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                  top: _isTop ? statusBarHeight : 0,
                  bottom: _isTop ? -statusBarHeight : 0,
                  left: 0,
                  right: 0,
                  child: Listener(
                      onPointerMove: (e) {
                        if (e.delta.dy < -5 && _isTop) setState(() => _isTop = false);
                        else if (e.delta.dy > 5 && !_isTop) setState(() => _isTop = true);
                      },
                      child: PDF(
                          swipeHorizontal: false,
                          autoSpacing: false,
                          pageFling: false,
                          pageSnap: false,
                          fitEachPage: true,
                          onPageChanged: (p, t) {
                            if (p == 0 && !_isTop) setState(() => _isTop = true);
                          }
                      ).cachedFromUrl(
                          widget.url,
                          headers: {'Cookie': widget.cookie},
                          placeholder: (p) => Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: AppTheme.textMain(context))),
                          errorWidget: (e) => Center(child: Icon(Icons.error_outline, color: AppTheme.textMain(context)))
                      )
                  )
              ),
              AnimatedPositioned(
                  duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                  curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                  top: _isTop ? statusBarHeight + 8 : -100,
                  right: 16,
                  child: Container(
                      decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                      child: IconButton(
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.download, color: Colors.white),
                          onPressed: () async {
                            try {
                              final uri = Uri.tryParse(widget.url);
                              if (uri != null && await canLaunchUrl(uri)) await launchUrl(uri, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
                            } catch (e) {}
                          }
                      )
                  )
              )
            ]
        )
    );
  }
}


class VideoViewerScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String url;
  final String cookie;
  const VideoViewerScreen({super.key, required this.url, required this.cookie});
  @override
  State<VideoViewerScreen> createState() => _VideoViewerScreenState();
}


class _VideoViewerScreenState extends State<VideoViewerScreen> {
  bool _isTop = true;
  InAppWebViewController? _webViewController;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _webViewController?.clearCache();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final double statusBarHeight = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top;



    return PopScope(
        canPop: false,
        onPopInvoked: (didPop) async {
          if (didPop) return;
          if (_webViewController != null && await _webViewController!.canGoBack()) {
            _webViewController!.goBack(); // Web縺ｮ螻･豁ｴ縺後≠繧後・謌ｻ繧・
          } else {
            if (context.mounted) Navigator.pop(context); // 縺ｪ縺代ｌ縺ｰ逕ｻ髱｢繧帝哩縺倥ｋ
          }
        },
        child: Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.bg(context),
          body: Stack(
            children: [
              AnimatedPositioned(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                top: _isTop ? statusBarHeight : 0,
                bottom: _isTop ? -statusBarHeight : 0,
                left: 0,
                right: 0,
                child: Listener(
                  onPointerMove: (e) {
                    if (e.delta.dy < -5 && _isTop) setState(() => _isTop = false);
                    else if (e.delta.dy > 5 && !_isTop) setState(() => _isTop = true);
                  },
                  // 1. InAppBrowserScreen 縺ｮ InAppWebView 繧剃ｻ･荳九・繧医≧縺ｫ菫ｮ豁｣縺励∪縺・
                  child: InAppWebView(
                    onWebViewCreated: (controller) => _webViewController = controller,
                    // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・壹け繝・く繝ｼ繧偵・繝・ム繝ｼ縺ｫ豕ｨ蜈･
                    initialUrlRequest: URLRequest(
                      url: WebUri(widget.url),
                      headers: {'Cookie': widget.cookie},
                    ),
                    initialSettings: InAppWebViewSettings(
                      javaScriptEnabled: true,
                      domStorageEnabled: true,
                      // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・壹け繝・く繝ｼ縺ｮ蜈ｱ譛芽ｨｭ螳・
                      sharedCookiesEnabled: true,
                      thirdPartyCookiesEnabled: true,
                      mediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture: true,
                      allowsInlineMediaPlayback: true,
                    ),
                    onScrollChanged: (c, x, y) {
                      if (y <= 0 && !_isTop) setState(() => _isTop = true);
                      else if (y > 5 && _isTop) setState(() => _isTop = false);
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        )
    );
  }
}


Future<void> openSmartUrl(BuildContext context, String url, String cookie, {String? title}) async {
  try {
    String fullUrl = url.startsWith('/') ? '$canvasBaseUrl$url' : url;
    if (Uri.tryParse(fullUrl) == null) return;


    String checkStr = (fullUrl + (title ?? '')).toLowerCase();


    // 笘・蜍慕判縺縺代〒縺ｪ縺上＾ffice邉ｻ繝峨く繝･繝｡繝ｳ繝茨ｼ・PTX, DOCX, XLSX・峨ｂWebView縺ｸ騾・′縺・
    bool isMediaOrDoc = checkStr.contains('.mp4') || checkStr.contains('.mov') || checkStr.contains('.webm') ||
        checkStr.contains('.pptx') || checkStr.contains('.ppt') ||
        checkStr.contains('.docx') || checkStr.contains('.doc') ||
        checkStr.contains('.xlsx') || checkStr.contains('.xls');


    if (isMediaOrDoc) {
      // WebView縺後ヵ繝ｪ繝ｼ繧ｺ・亥ｼｷ蛻ｶ菫晏ｭ假ｼ峨＠縺ｪ縺・ｈ縺・∫ｴ皮ｲ九↑Canvas繝励Ξ繝薙Η繝ｼURL縺ｫ螟画鋤縺吶ｋ
      String safeUrl = fullUrl.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[?&]download_frd=1'), '');
      if (safeUrl.endsWith('/download')) {
        safeUrl = safeUrl.substring(0, safeUrl.length - 9);
      }
      // 蜑榊屓繧ｷ繝ｳ繝励Ν縺ｫ縺励◆VideoViewerScreen・亥ｮ牙・縺ｪWebView・峨∈騾√ｋ
      Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => VideoViewerScreen(url: safeUrl, cookie: cookie)));
    }
    // 笘・PDF縺ｮ蝣ｴ蜷茨ｼ井ｻ翫∪縺ｧ騾壹ｊ蟆ら畑縺ｮ鬮倬溘ン繝･繝ｼ繧｢縺ｸ・・
    else if (fullUrl.toLowerCase().contains('.pdf') || fullUrl.contains('download_frd=1')) {
      if (fullUrl.contains('/files/') && !fullUrl.contains('download')) {
        fullUrl = "${fullUrl.split('?').first}/download?download_frd=1";
      }
      Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => PdfViewerScreen(url: fullUrl, cookie: cookie)));
    }
    // 笘・縺昴・莉悶・蝣ｴ蜷・
    else {
      Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => InAppBrowserScreen(url: fullUrl, cookie: cookie)));
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}
