class TermCirclePainter extends CustomPainter {
  final bool q1Active;
  final bool q2Active;
  final bool q3Active;
  final bool q4Active;
  final Color activeColor;
  final Color inactiveColor;


  TermCirclePainter(this.q1Active, this.q2Active, this.q3Active, this.q4Active, this.activeColor, this.inactiveColor);


  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final Paint paint = Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 2.0 // 讌ｵ邏ｰ縺ｮ蜀・腸
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;


    final center = Offset(size.width / 2, size.height / 2);
    final radius = size.width / 2 - 10;
    final double gap = 0.2;
    final double sweep = (math.pi / 2) - gap;


    // Q1 (蜿ｳ荳・
    paint.color = q1Active ? activeColor : inactiveColor;
    canvas.drawArc(Rect.fromCircle(center: center, radius: radius), gap / 2, sweep, false, paint);


    // Q2 (蟾ｦ荳・
    paint.color = q2Active ? activeColor : inactiveColor;
    canvas.drawArc(Rect.fromCircle(center: center, radius: radius), math.pi / 2 + gap / 2, sweep, false, paint);


    // Q3 (蟾ｦ荳・
    paint.color = q3Active ? activeColor : inactiveColor;
    canvas.drawArc(Rect.fromCircle(center: center, radius: radius), math.pi + gap / 2, sweep, false, paint);


    // Q4 (蜿ｳ荳・
    paint.color = q4Active ? activeColor : inactiveColor;
    canvas.drawArc(Rect.fromCircle(center: center, radius: radius), 3 * math.pi / 2 + gap / 2, sweep, false, paint);
  }


  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant TermCirclePainter oldDelegate) {
    return oldDelegate.q1Active != q1Active ||
        oldDelegate.q2Active != q2Active ||
        oldDelegate.q3Active != q3Active ||
        oldDelegate.q4Active != q4Active;
  }
}


class YearCirclePainter extends CustomPainter {
  final double pulseValue;
  final bool isClockwise;
  final Color activeColor;
  final Color inactiveColor;


  YearCirclePainter(this.pulseValue, this.isClockwise, this.activeColor, this.inactiveColor);


  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final Paint basePaint = Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 2.0 // 讌ｵ邏ｰ邱・
      ..color = inactiveColor
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;


    final center = Offset(size.width / 2, size.height / 2);
    final radius = size.width / 2 - 10;


    // 蛻・牡縺ｮ縺ｪ縺・蜻ｨ縺ｮ繝吶・繧ｹ繝ｩ繧､繝ｳ
    canvas.drawCircle(center, radius, basePaint);


    // 繝代Ν繧ｹ繧｢繝九Γ繝ｼ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ (0.0 縲・1.0 縺ｧ1蜻ｨ)
    if (pulseValue > 0.0 && pulseValue < 1.0) {
      canvas.save();
      canvas.translate(center.dx, center.dy);


      // 繧｢繝九Γ繝ｼ繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ縺ｮ騾ｲ陦後↓蜷医ｏ縺帙※繧ｭ繝｣繝ｳ繝舌せ繧貞屓霆｢
      double currentAngle = -math.pi / 2 + (isClockwise ? pulseValue * 2 * math.pi : -pulseValue * 2 * math.pi);
      canvas.rotate(currentAngle);


      final Rect localRect = Rect.fromCircle(center: Offset.zero, radius: radius);


      // 繧ｵ繧､繝ｳ豕｢縺ｧ繝輔ぉ繝ｼ繝峨う繝ｳ繝ｻ繝輔ぉ繝ｼ繝峨い繧ｦ繝医☆繧倶ｸ埼乗・蠎ｦ
      final double opacity = math.sin(pulseValue * math.pi);


      // 鄒弱＠縺・げ繝ｩ繝・・繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ縺ｮ蟆ｾ繧貞ｼ輔￥繝代Ν繧ｹ
      final Gradient gradient = SweepGradient(
        colors: isClockwise
            ? [activeColor.withOpacity(0.0), activeColor.withOpacity(0.0), activeColor.withOpacity(opacity)]
            : [activeColor.withOpacity(opacity), activeColor.withOpacity(0.0), activeColor.withOpacity(0.0)],
        stops: const [0.0, 0.5, 1.0],
      );


      final Paint pulsePaint = Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = 3.0
        ..shader = gradient.createShader(localRect)
        ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;


      // 蝗櫁ｻ｢縺励◆繧ｭ繝｣繝ｳ繝舌せ荳翫↓蜀・ｒ謠冗判縺吶ｋ縺薙→縺ｧ縲√げ繝ｩ繝・・繧ｷ繝ｧ繝ｳ縺悟屓縺｣縺ｦ縺・ｋ繧医≧縺ｫ隕九○繧・
      canvas.drawCircle(Offset.zero, radius, pulsePaint);
      canvas.restore();
    }
  }


  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant YearCirclePainter oldDelegate) {
    return oldDelegate.pulseValue != pulseValue || oldDelegate.isClockwise != isClockwise;
  }
}


class SettingsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final int currentYear;
  final String currentTerm;
  final bool isAuthenticated;
  final VoidCallback onLogin;
  final VoidCallback onLogout;
  final Function(int, String) onApply;

  const SettingsScreen({super.key, required this.currentYear, required this.currentTerm, required this.isAuthenticated, required this.onLogin, required this.onLogout, required this.onApply});
  @override
  State<SettingsScreen> createState() => _SettingsScreenState();
}


class _SettingsScreenState extends State<SettingsScreen> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late int tempYear;
  late String tempTerm;
  late bool _isAuthenticated;

  late AnimationController _pulseController;
  bool _isClockwise = true;

  // 笘・繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ蜷梧悄逕ｨ繧ｹ繝・・繝茨ｼ・縺､縺ｮ鬆・岼縺ｫ諡｡蠑ｵ・・
  bool _isSyncEnabled = false;
  String? _targetCalendarId;
  List<dc.Calendar> _availableCalendars = [];

  bool _syncTasksEnabled = false;
  bool _syncSchedulesEnabled = false;
  bool _importEventsEnabled = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    tempYear = widget.currentYear;
    tempTerm = widget.currentTerm;
    _isAuthenticated = widget.isAuthenticated;
    _pulseController = AnimationController(vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 600));
    _loadSyncSettings();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pulseController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadSyncSettings() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    setState(() {
      _isSyncEnabled = prefs.getBool('calendar_sync_enabled') ?? false;
      _targetCalendarId = prefs.getString('calendar_sync_target_id');
      _syncTasksEnabled = prefs.getBool('sync_tasks_enabled') ?? false;
      _syncSchedulesEnabled = prefs.getBool('sync_schedules_enabled') ?? false;
      _importEventsEnabled = prefs.getBool('sync_import_enabled') ?? false;
    });
    if (_isSyncEnabled) {
      await _fetchCalendars();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _fetchCalendars() async {
    final cals = await CalendarSyncService.getWritableCalendars();
    setState(() {
      _availableCalendars = cals;
      if (_targetCalendarId != null && !_availableCalendars.any((c) => c.id == _targetCalendarId)) {
        _targetCalendarId = null;
      }
    });
  }

  // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・壼句挨縺ｮ蜷梧悄繧ｹ繧､繝・メ縺ｮ繝上Φ繝峨Λ繝ｼ
  Future<void> _toggleSyncTasks(bool value) async {
    setState(() => _syncTasksEnabled = value);
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('sync_tasks_enabled', value);
    if (!value) {
      await CalendarSyncService.disableTaskSync(); // 蜈ｱ譛牙●豁｢縺ｧ蜊ｳ蠎ｧ縺ｫ蜑企勁
    } else {
      // ON縺ｫ縺励◆蝣ｴ蜷医∵ｬ｡蝗槭い繝励Μ蜀崎ｵｷ蜍墓凾繧・酔譛溘・繧ｿ繝ｳ謚ｼ荳区凾縺ｫ譖ｸ縺榊・縺輔ｌ繧・
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('繧ｿ繧ｹ繧ｯ蜷梧悄繧呈怏蜉ｹ縺ｫ縺励∪縺励◆縲よｬ｡蝗樊峩譁ｰ譎ゅ↓蜿肴丐縺輔ｌ縺ｾ縺吶・)));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _toggleSyncSchedules(bool value) async {
    setState(() => _syncSchedulesEnabled = value);
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('sync_schedules_enabled', value);
    if (!value) {
      await CalendarSyncService.disableScheduleSync(); // 蜈ｱ譛牙●豁｢縺ｧ蜊ｳ蠎ｧ縺ｫ蜑企勁
    } else {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('繧ｹ繧ｱ繧ｸ繝･繝ｼ繝ｫ蜷梧悄繧呈怏蜉ｹ縺ｫ縺励∪縺励◆縲よｬ｡蝗樊峩譁ｰ譎ゅ↓蜿肴丐縺輔ｌ縺ｾ縺吶・)));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _toggleImportEvents(bool value) async {
    setState(() => _importEventsEnabled = value);
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('sync_import_enabled', value);
  }

  // 繝槭せ繧ｿ繝ｼ讖溯・縺ｮ繝医げ繝ｫ
  Future<void> _toggleSync(bool value) async {
    if (value) {
      bool granted = await CalendarSyncService.requestPermissions();
      if (!granted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ縺ｸ縺ｮ繧｢繧ｯ繧ｻ繧ｹ縺瑚ｨｱ蜿ｯ縺輔ｌ縺ｦ縺・∪縺帙ｓ')));
        return;
      }
      await _fetchCalendars();
    }

    setState(() {
      _isSyncEnabled = value;
      if (!value) {
        _targetCalendarId = null;
        _syncTasksEnabled = false;
        _syncSchedulesEnabled = false;
        _importEventsEnabled = false;
      }
    });

    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('calendar_sync_enabled', _isSyncEnabled);
    if (!value) {
      await prefs.remove('calendar_sync_target_id');
      await prefs.setBool('sync_tasks_enabled', false);
      await prefs.setBool('sync_schedules_enabled', false);
      await prefs.setBool('sync_import_enabled', false);
      // 繧ｪ繝輔↓縺励◆蝣ｴ蜷医・蜈ｨ繝・・繧ｿ繧剃ｸ諡ｬ蜑企勁
      await CalendarSyncService.disableTaskSync();
      await CalendarSyncService.disableScheduleSync();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _selectTargetCalendar(String? calId) async {
    if (calId == null) return;
    setState(() => _targetCalendarId = calId);
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setString('calendar_sync_target_id', calId);
  }

  bool get q1 => tempTerm.contains('譏･') || tempTerm.contains('Q1') || tempTerm.contains('騾壼ｹｴ');
  bool get q2 => tempTerm.contains('譏･') || tempTerm.contains('Q2') || tempTerm.contains('騾壼ｹｴ');
  bool get q3 => tempTerm.contains('遘・) || tempTerm.contains('Q3') || tempTerm.contains('騾壼ｹｴ');
  bool get q4 => tempTerm.contains('遘・) || tempTerm.contains('Q4') || tempTerm.contains('騾壼ｹｴ');

  void _update() { widget.onApply(tempYear, tempTerm); }

  // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・壽焔蜍輔く繝｣繝・す繝･繧ｯ繝ｪ繧｢・・ookie縺ｯ邯ｭ謖√＠縺ｦ繝ｭ繧ｰ繧｢繧ｦ繝医ｒ髦ｲ縺弱▽縺､縲・㍾縺・ョ繝ｼ繧ｿ縺縺代ｒ謐ｨ縺ｦ繧具ｼ・
  // 笘・遨ｶ讌ｵ縺ｮ繝・ぅ繝ｼ繝励け繝ｪ繝ｼ繝ｳ・・ｮｹ驥上せ繧ｭ繝｣繝ｳ讖溯・
  // 笘・譛ｬ逡ｪ逕ｨ縺ｮ螳牙・縺ｪ繧ｭ繝｣繝・す繝･繧ｯ繝ｪ繧｢・郁ｨｭ螳夂判髱｢縺ｮ謇句虚繝懊ち繝ｳ逕ｨ・・
  Future<void> _clearAppCache() async {
    // 1. WebView縺ｮ繧ｭ繝｣繝・す繝･繧偵け繝ｪ繧｢・・ookie縺ｯ邯ｭ謖・ｼ・
    try {

    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Manual Cache Clear Error (WebView): $e");
    }

    // 2. OS縺ｮ荳譎ゅョ繧｣繝ｬ繧ｯ繝医Μ・・emp・峨↓縺ゅｋ荳崎ｦ√↑繝輔ぃ繧､繝ｫ繧剃ｸ謗・
    // 窶ｻ驥大ｺｫ驛ｨ螻具ｼ・ocuments・峨↓縺ｯ隗ｦ繧後↑縺・・縺ｧ螳牙・縺ｧ縺・
    try {
      final tempDir = await getTemporaryDirectory();
      if (tempDir.existsSync()) {
        tempDir.listSync().forEach((file) {
          try {
            if (file is File) {
              file.deleteSync();
            }
          } catch (_) {}
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("Manual Cache Clear Error (Temp files): $e");
    }

    // 3. 繝ｦ繝ｼ繧ｶ繝ｼ縺ｸ螳御ｺ・ｒ騾夂衍
    if (mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(content: Text('繧ｭ繝｣繝・す繝･繧偵け繝ｪ繧｢縺励∪縺励◆・育ｩｺ縺榊ｮｹ驥上ｒ遒ｺ菫昴＠縺ｾ縺励◆・・))
      );
    }
  }

  void _animateYear(bool increase) {
    setState(() { if (increase) { tempYear++; _isClockwise = true; } else { tempYear--; _isClockwise = false; } _update(); });
    _pulseController.forward(from: 0.0);
  }

  void _handleCircleTap(Offset localPosition, Size size) {
    final center = Offset(size.width / 2, size.height / 2);
    final angle = math.atan2(localPosition.dy - center.dy, localPosition.dx - center.dx);
    bool nq1 = q1, nq2 = q2, nq3 = q3, nq4 = q4;
    setState(() {
      if (angle >= 0 && angle < math.pi / 2) nq1 = !nq1;
      else if (angle >= math.pi / 2 && angle <= math.pi) nq2 = !nq2;
      else if (angle >= -math.pi && angle < -math.pi / 2) nq3 = !nq3;
      else nq4 = !nq4;
      if (nq1 && nq2 && nq3 && nq4) tempTerm = "騾壼ｹｴ";
      else if (nq1 && nq2 && !nq3 && !nq4) tempTerm = "譏･蟄ｦ譛・;
      else if (!nq1 && !nq2 && nq3 && nq4) tempTerm = "遘句ｭｦ譛・;
      else {
        List<String> parts = [];
        if (nq1) parts.add("Q1"); if (nq2) parts.add("Q2"); if (nq3) parts.add("Q3"); if (nq4) parts.add("Q4");
        tempTerm = parts.isEmpty ? "譛ｪ驕ｸ謚・ : parts.join(".");
      }
      _update();
    });
  }

  // 繧ｵ繝悶ヨ繧ｰ繝ｫ繧呈緒逕ｻ縺吶ｋ繝倥Ν繝代・
  Widget _buildSyncSubToggle(String title, bool value, Function(bool) onChanged) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20.0, vertical: 12.0),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(title, style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w300)),
          SizedBox(
            height: 20,
            child: Transform.scale(
              scale: 0.75,
              child: Switch(
                value: value,
                onChanged: (v) {
                  // 繧ｿ繝ｼ繧ｲ繝・ヨ繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ縺梧悴驕ｸ謚槭・蝣ｴ蜷医・譖ｸ縺榊・縺励ｒ險ｱ蜿ｯ縺励↑縺・
                  if (_targetCalendarId == null && title.contains('Export')) {
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('Please select a target calendar first.')));
                    return;
                  }
                  onChanged(v);
                },
                activeColor: AppTheme.textMain(context),
                activeTrackColor: AppTheme.textMain(context).withOpacity(0.5),
                inactiveThumbColor: AppTheme.textSub(context),
                inactiveTrackColor: Colors.transparent,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

                        Navigator.push(

  }

  Widget _buildArrowBtn(IconData icon, VoidCallback onTap) {
    return GestureDetector(
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8.0, vertical: 0.0),
        child: Icon(icon, size: 28, color: AppTheme.textMain(context).withOpacity(0.2)),
      ),
    );
  }

  // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・喇ideArrow 繝輔Λ繧ｰ繧定ｿｽ蜉・医ョ繝輔か繝ｫ繝医・ false 縺ｪ縺ｮ縺ｧ譌｢蟄倥さ繝ｼ繝峨↓蠖ｱ髻ｿ縺ｪ縺暦ｼ・
  Widget _buildCustomListItem(String title, {String? trailingText, VoidCallback? onTap, bool hideArrow = false}) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20.0, vertical: 16.0),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: [
            Text(title, style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textMain(context), fontWeight: FontWeight.w300)),
            if (trailingText != null)
              Text(trailingText, style: TextStyle(color: AppTheme.textSub(context), fontWeight: FontWeight.w300, fontSize: 14))
            else if (onTap != null && !hideArrow) // 笘・菫ｮ豁｣・喇ideArrow 縺・true 縺ｪ繧臥泙蜊ｰ繧貞・縺輔↑縺・
              Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios, size: 14, color: AppTheme.textSub(context)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildThemeSegment(String title, ThemeMode mode) {
    bool isSelected = appThemeNotifier.value == mode;
    return Expanded(
        child: GestureDetector(
            behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
            onTap: () async {
              appThemeNotifier.value = mode;
              final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
              if (mode == ThemeMode.system) prefs.remove('app_theme_mode');
              else prefs.setString('app_theme_mode', mode == ThemeMode.light ? 'light' : 'dark');
              setState(() {});
            },
            child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: isSelected ? AppTheme.textMain(context).withOpacity(0.1) : Colors.transparent,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22)
                ),
                child: Center(
                  child: Text(title, style: TextStyle(
                      color: isSelected ? AppTheme.textMain(context) : AppTheme.textSub(context),
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal
                  )),
                )
            )
        )
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart'; // 竊・debugPrint逕ｨ縺ｫ霑ｽ蜉
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart' as fss;
import 'package:flutter_inappwebview/flutter_inappwebview.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import '../utils/lms_adapter.dart';
//auth_service.dart

class AuthService {
  static const _storage = fss.FlutterSecureStorage(
    aOptions: fss.AndroidOptions(encryptedSharedPreferences: true),
  );
  static const String _cookieKey = 'canvas_cookie';

  static Future<void> saveSession(String cookie) async {
    try {
      await _storage.write(key: _cookieKey, value: cookie);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("AuthService.saveSession Error: $e");
    }
  }

  static Future<String?> getSession() async {
    try {
      return await _storage.read(key: _cookieKey);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("AuthService.getSession Error: $e");
      return null;
    }
  }

  static Future<void> clearSession() async {
    try {
      await _storage.delete(key: _cookieKey);
      await CookieManager.instance().deleteCookies(url: WebUri(canvasBaseUrl));
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("AuthService.clearSession Error: $e");
    }
  }

  static Future<bool> validateSession(String cookie) async {
    try {
      final uri = Uri.tryParse('$canvasBaseUrl/api/v1/users/self');
      if (uri == null) return false;
      final response = await http.get(uri, headers: {'Cookie': cookie}).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 10)); // 竊・繧ｿ繧､繝繧｢繧ｦ繝郁ｿｽ蜉
      return response.statusCode == 200;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("AuthService.validateSession Error: $e");
      return false;
    }
  }
}
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart'; // 竊・debugPrint逕ｨ縺ｫ霑ｽ蜉
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
//cache_manager.dart

class CacheManager {
  static Future<void> save(String key, dynamic data) async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setString(key, jsonEncode({'timestamp': DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch, 'data': data}));
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("CacheManager.save Error [$key]: $e");
    }
  }

  static Future<dynamic> load(String key) async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final str = prefs.getString(key);
      if (str == null) return null;
      final decoded = jsonDecode(str);
      if (decoded is Map && decoded.containsKey('data')) return decoded['data'];
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("CacheManager.load Error [$key]: $e");
    }
    return null;
  }
}
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:device_calendar/device_calendar.dart' as dc;
import '../utils/lms_adapter.dart'; // currentLMS 繧剃ｽｿ縺・◆繧√↓蠢・ｦ・
//calendar_sync_service.dart

// ----------------------------------------------------
// 笘・繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ蜷梧悄繧ｵ繝ｼ繝薙せ v7 (邨ｶ蟇ｾ遒ｺ螳溘・繧ｯ繝ｪ繝ｼ繝ｳ繧｢繝・・螳悟ｙ迚・
// ----------------------------------------------------
class CalendarSyncService {
  static final dc.DeviceCalendarPlugin _deviceCalendarPlugin = dc.DeviceCalendarPlugin();

  static Future<bool> requestPermissions() async {
    var permissionsGranted = await _deviceCalendarPlugin.hasPermissions();
    if (permissionsGranted.isSuccess && !permissionsGranted.data!) {
      permissionsGranted = await _deviceCalendarPlugin.requestPermissions();
      if (!permissionsGranted.isSuccess || !permissionsGranted.data!) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  static Future<List<dc.Calendar>> getWritableCalendars() async {
    if (!await requestPermissions()) return <dc.Calendar>[];
    final calendarsResult = await _deviceCalendarPlugin.retrieveCalendars();
    return (calendarsResult.data ?? []).cast<dc.Calendar>().where((c) => c.isReadOnly == false).toList();
  }

  // 笘・蜊倅ｸ繧ｿ繧ｹ繧ｯ縺ｮ蜊ｳ譎ょ炎髯､・亥ｮ御ｺ・ム繧､繧｢繝ｭ繧ｰ遲峨°繧牙他縺ｰ繧後ｋ・・
  static Future<void> deleteTaskFromCalendar(String taskId) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final String? targetCalendarId = prefs.getString('calendar_sync_target_id');
    if (targetCalendarId == null || targetCalendarId.isEmpty) return;

    Map<String, String> eventMapping = Map<String, String>.from(jsonDecode(prefs.getString('cal_map_tasks') ?? '{}'));

    if (eventMapping.containsKey(taskId)) {
      await _deviceCalendarPlugin.deleteEvent(targetCalendarId, eventMapping[taskId]);
      eventMapping.remove(taskId);
      await prefs.setString('cal_map_tasks', jsonEncode(eventMapping));
    }
  }

  // ==========================================
  // 1. TASKS -> 繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ 蜷梧悄
  // ==========================================
  static Future<void> syncTasks(List<dynamic> tasks) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    if (!(prefs.getBool('sync_tasks_enabled') ?? false)) return;
    final targetCalId = prefs.getString('calendar_sync_target_id');
    if (targetCalId == null || targetCalId.isEmpty || !await requestPermissions()) return;

    Map<String, String> eventMapping = Map<String, String>.from(jsonDecode(prefs.getString('cal_map_tasks') ?? '{}'));
    List<String> dismissedIds = prefs.getStringList('dismissed_tasks_v10') ?? [];
    List<String> activeTaskIds = [];

    for (var task in tasks) {
      String taskId = task['id'].toString();
      String rawDue = task['due_at'] ?? '';
      if (rawDue.isEmpty) continue;

      DateTime? dueAt = DateTime.tryParse(rawDue)?.toLocal();
      if (dueAt == null) continue;

      bool isCompleted = false;
      if (task['course_id'] == 'custom_t' || task['is_app_custom_task'] == true) {
        isCompleted = task['is_completed'] == true;
      } else {
        final sub = task['submission'];
        isCompleted = sub != null && (sub['workflow_state'] == 'submitted' || sub['workflow_state'] == 'graded' || sub['workflow_state'] == 'graded_excused');
      }

      // 笘・隕∽ｻｶ繧ｯ繝ｪ繧｢・壼ｮ御ｺ・＠縺溘ち繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｻ髱櫁｡ｨ遉ｺ繧ｿ繧ｹ繧ｯ繝ｻ驕主悉縺ｮ繧ｿ繧ｹ繧ｯ縺ｯOS繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ縺九ｉ蜑企勁
      if (isCompleted || dismissedIds.contains(taskId) || dueAt.isBefore(DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 1)))) {
        if (eventMapping.containsKey(taskId)) {
          await _deviceCalendarPlugin.deleteEvent(targetCalId, eventMapping[taskId]);
          eventMapping.remove(taskId);
        }
        continue; // 逋ｻ骭ｲ蜃ｦ逅・・繧ｹ繧ｭ繝・・
      }

      activeTaskIds.add(taskId);
      dc.Event event = dc.Event(targetCalId);

      // 譌｢蟄露D縺後≠繧後・荳頑嶌縺搾ｼ・psert・・
      if (eventMapping.containsKey(taskId)) {
        event.eventId = eventMapping[taskId];
      }

      event.title = "[隱ｲ鬘珪 ${task['name'] ?? 'Untitled'}";
      event.description = "${currentLMS.cleanTitle(task['context_name'] ?? '')}\n${task['html_url'] ?? task['url'] ?? ''}";

      event.start = dc.TZDateTime.from(dueAt.subtract(const Duration(hours: 1)), dc.local);
      event.end = dc.TZDateTime.from(dueAt, dc.local);

      final result = await _deviceCalendarPlugin.createOrUpdateEvent(event);
      if (result?.isSuccess == true && result?.data != null) {
        eventMapping[taskId] = result!.data!;
      }
    }

    // 繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ縺ｫ谿九▲縺ｦ縺・ｋ縺後√い繝励Μ蛛ｴ縺九ｉ豸医∴縺溘ち繧ｹ繧ｯ繧呈祉髯､
    List<String> toRemove = [];
    for (var key in eventMapping.keys) {
      if (!activeTaskIds.contains(key)) {
        await _deviceCalendarPlugin.deleteEvent(targetCalId, eventMapping[key]);
        toRemove.add(key);
      }
    }
    toRemove.forEach((k) => eventMapping.remove(k));
    await prefs.setString('cal_map_tasks', jsonEncode(eventMapping));
  }

  // 笘・隕∽ｻｶ繧ｯ繝ｪ繧｢・壹ち繧ｹ繧ｯ蜷梧悄繧ｪ繝墓凾縺ｫ螳悟・荳謗・☆繧・
  static Future<void> disableTaskSync() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final targetCalId = prefs.getString('calendar_sync_target_id');
    if (targetCalId == null || targetCalId.isEmpty) return;

    // 1. 繝槭ャ繝励↓險倬鹸縺輔ｌ縺櫑D繝吶・繧ｹ縺ｧ蜑企勁
    Map<String, String> eventMapping = Map<String, String>.from(jsonDecode(prefs.getString('cal_map_tasks') ?? '{}'));
    for (var eventId in eventMapping.values) {
      await _deviceCalendarPlugin.deleteEvent(targetCalId, eventId);
    }
    await prefs.remove('cal_map_tasks');
    await prefs.setBool('sync_tasks_enabled', false);

    // 2. 繝輔ぉ繧､繝ｫ繧ｻ繝ｼ繝包ｼ郁ｨ倬鹸貍上ｌ縺ｮ [隱ｲ鬘珪 繧呈､懃ｴ｢縺励※蠑ｷ蛻ｶ荳謗・ｼ・
    final startDate = dc.TZDateTime.local(DateTime.now().year - 1, 1, 1);
    final endDate = dc.TZDateTime.local(DateTime.now().year + 2, 1, 1);
    final eventsResult = await _deviceCalendarPlugin.retrieveEvents(targetCalId, dc.RetrieveEventsParams(startDate: startDate, endDate: endDate));
    if (eventsResult.isSuccess && eventsResult.data != null) {
      for (var e in eventsResult.data!) {
        if (e.title != null && e.title!.startsWith('[隱ｲ鬘珪')) {
          await _deviceCalendarPlugin.deleteEvent(targetCalId, e.eventId);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ==========================================
  // 2. SCHEDULES -> 繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ 蜷梧悄
  // ==========================================
  static Future<void> syncSchedules(List<dynamic> courses, int year, String term) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    if (!(prefs.getBool('sync_schedules_enabled') ?? false)) return;
    final targetCalId = prefs.getString('calendar_sync_target_id');
    if (targetCalId == null || targetCalId.isEmpty || !await requestPermissions()) return;

    Map<String, String> eventMapping = Map<String, String>.from(jsonDecode(prefs.getString('cal_map_schedules') ?? '{}'));
    List<String> activeScheduleIds = [];

    DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    bool isSpring = term.contains('譏･') || term.contains('Q1') || term.contains('Q2') || term.contains('蜑肴悄') || term.contains('SPRING');
    DateTime semStart = currentLMS.getSemesterStartDate(now, isSpring);
    DateTime semEnd = currentLMS.getSemesterEndDate(now, isSpring);

    if (term.contains('Q1') || term.contains('Q3')) {
      semEnd = semStart.add(Duration(days: (semEnd.difference(semStart).inDays / 2).floor()));
    } else if (term.contains('Q2') || term.contains('Q4')) {
      semStart = semStart.add(Duration(days: (semEnd.difference(semStart).inDays / 2).floor()));
    }

    final Map<int, Map<String, int>> periods = {
      0: {'start': 900, 'end': 1030}, 1: {'start': 1045, 'end': 1215}, 2: {'start': 1300, 'end': 1430},
      3: {'start': 1445, 'end': 1615}, 4: {'start': 1630, 'end': 1800}, 5: {'start': 1815, 'end': 1945}
    };

    for (var c in courses) {
      String courseId = c['is_custom'] == true ? (c['raw_name'] ?? c['name'] ?? '') : c['id'].toString();
      String rawName = c['raw_name'] ?? c['name'] ?? '';

      if (c['is_custom'] == true && c['is_weekly'] == false) continue;

      List<Map<String, int>> schedules = c['is_custom'] == true ? [{'day': c['day'], 'period': c['period']}] : currentLMS.parseWeeklySchedule(rawName);

      for (var s in schedules) {
        int dayIdx = s['day']!;
        int periodIdx = s['period']!;
        if (!periods.containsKey(periodIdx)) continue;

        String uniqueSchId = "${courseId}_${dayIdx}_$periodIdx";
        activeScheduleIds.add(uniqueSchId);

        DateTime firstClassDate = semStart;
        while (firstClassDate.weekday - 1 != dayIdx) {
          firstClassDate = firstClassDate.add(const Duration(days: 1));
        }
        if (firstClassDate.isAfter(semEnd)) continue;

        int startHour = periods[periodIdx]!['start']! ~/ 100;
        int startMin = periods[periodIdx]!['start']! % 100;
        int endHour = periods[periodIdx]!['end']! ~/ 100;
        int endMin = periods[periodIdx]!['end']! % 100;

        dc.Event event = dc.Event(targetCalId);

        if (eventMapping.containsKey(uniqueSchId)) {
          event.eventId = eventMapping[uniqueSchId];
        }

        String titleStr = currentLMS.cleanTitle(rawName);
        String roomStr = currentLMS.extractRoom(rawName);
        if (c['is_custom'] == true) { titleStr = rawName; roomStr = c['room'] ?? ''; }

        event.title = "[謗域･ｭ] $titleStr";
        event.description = "謨吝ｮ､: $roomStr\n繧｢繝励Μ縺九ｉ蜷梧悄縺輔ｌ縺溘せ繧ｱ繧ｸ繝･繝ｼ繝ｫ";
        event.location = roomStr;

        DateTime startDt = DateTime(firstClassDate.year, firstClassDate.month, firstClassDate.day, startHour, startMin);
        DateTime endDt = DateTime(firstClassDate.year, firstClassDate.month, firstClassDate.day, endHour, endMin);

        event.start = dc.TZDateTime.from(startDt, dc.local);
        event.end = dc.TZDateTime.from(endDt, dc.local);

        dc.RecurrenceRule rrule = dc.RecurrenceRule(dc.RecurrenceFrequency.Weekly);
        rrule.endDate = dc.TZDateTime.from(DateTime(semEnd.year, semEnd.month, semEnd.day, 23, 59), dc.local);
        event.recurrenceRule = rrule;

        final result = await _deviceCalendarPlugin.createOrUpdateEvent(event);
        if (result?.isSuccess == true && result?.data != null) {
          eventMapping[uniqueSchId] = result!.data!;
        }
      }
    }

    List<String> toRemove = [];
    for (var key in eventMapping.keys) {
      if (!activeScheduleIds.contains(key)) {
        await _deviceCalendarPlugin.deleteEvent(targetCalId, eventMapping[key]);
        toRemove.add(key);
      }
    }
    toRemove.forEach((k) => eventMapping.remove(k));
    await prefs.setString('cal_map_schedules', jsonEncode(eventMapping));
  }

  // 笘・隕∽ｻｶ繧ｯ繝ｪ繧｢・壹せ繧ｱ繧ｸ繝･繝ｼ繝ｫ蜷梧悄繧ｪ繝墓凾縺ｫ螳悟・荳謗・☆繧・
  static Future<void> disableScheduleSync() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final targetCalId = prefs.getString('calendar_sync_target_id');
    if (targetCalId == null || targetCalId.isEmpty) return;

    // 1. 繝槭ャ繝励↓險倬鹸縺輔ｌ縺櫑D繝吶・繧ｹ縺ｧ蜑企勁
    Map<String, String> eventMapping = Map<String, String>.from(jsonDecode(prefs.getString('cal_map_schedules') ?? '{}'));
    for (var eventId in eventMapping.values) {
      await _deviceCalendarPlugin.deleteEvent(targetCalId, eventId);
    }
    await prefs.remove('cal_map_schedules');
    await prefs.setBool('sync_schedules_enabled', false);

    // 2. 繝輔ぉ繧､繝ｫ繧ｻ繝ｼ繝包ｼ郁ｨ倬鹸貍上ｌ縺ｮ [謗域･ｭ] 繧呈､懃ｴ｢縺励※蠑ｷ蛻ｶ荳謗・ｼ・
    final startDate = dc.TZDateTime.local(DateTime.now().year - 1, 1, 1);
    final endDate = dc.TZDateTime.local(DateTime.now().year + 2, 1, 1);
    final eventsResult = await _deviceCalendarPlugin.retrieveEvents(targetCalId, dc.RetrieveEventsParams(startDate: startDate, endDate: endDate));
    if (eventsResult.isSuccess && eventsResult.data != null) {
      for (var e in eventsResult.data!) {
        if (e.title != null && e.title!.startsWith('[謗域･ｭ]')) {
          await _deviceCalendarPlugin.deleteEvent(targetCalId, e.eventId);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ==========================================
  // 3. 繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ -> 繧｢繝励Μ (Dynamic Read)
  // ==========================================
  static Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getDeviceEventsForDate(DateTime date) async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    if (!(prefs.getBool('sync_import_enabled') ?? false)) return [];
    if (!await requestPermissions()) return [];

    Map<String, String> appTaskMap = Map<String, String>.from(jsonDecode(prefs.getString('cal_map_tasks') ?? '{}'));
    Map<String, String> appScheduleMap = Map<String, String>.from(jsonDecode(prefs.getString('cal_map_schedules') ?? '{}'));
    List<String> masterIds = [...appTaskMap.values, ...appScheduleMap.values];

    List<Map<String, dynamic>> externalEvents = [];
    final calendars = await getWritableCalendars();

    final startDate = dc.TZDateTime(dc.local, date.year, date.month, date.day);
    final endDate = startDate.add(const Duration(days: 1));

    // 笘・霑ｽ蜉・夊､・焚縺ｮ繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ髢薙〒蜷後§莠亥ｮ壹′繝繝悶ｋ縺ｮ繧帝亟縺舌そ繝・ヨ
    Set<String> uniqueSignatures = {};

    for (var cal in calendars) {
      final eventsResult = await _deviceCalendarPlugin.retrieveEvents(cal.id, dc.RetrieveEventsParams(startDate: startDate, endDate: endDate));
      if (eventsResult.isSuccess && eventsResult.data != null) {
        for (var e in eventsResult.data!) {
          String currentEventId = e.eventId ?? '';

          if (masterIds.contains(currentEventId)) continue;
          bool isInstance = masterIds.any((id) => currentEventId.startsWith(id));
          if (isInstance) continue;
          if (e.title != null && (e.title!.startsWith('[隱ｲ鬘珪') || e.title!.startsWith('[謗域･ｭ]'))) continue;

          bool isAllDay = e.allDay ?? false;
          DateTime eventStart;
          DateTime eventEnd; // 笘・霑ｽ蜉
          if (e.start != null && e.end != null) {
            eventStart = DateTime(e.start!.year, e.start!.month, e.start!.day, e.start!.hour, e.start!.minute);
            eventEnd = DateTime(e.end!.year, e.end!.month, e.end!.day, e.end!.hour, e.end!.minute);
          } else {
            continue;
          }

          if (isAllDay) {
            eventStart = DateTime(date.year, date.month, date.day, 0, 0);
            eventEnd = DateTime(date.year, date.month, date.day, 23, 59);
          }

          // 笘・蜷御ｸ繧ｿ繧､繝医Ν繝ｻ蜷御ｸ髢句ｧ区凾髢薙・莠亥ｮ壹・1縺､縺ｫ縺ｾ縺ｨ繧√ｋ・医ム繝悶ｊ髦ｲ豁｢・・
          String signature = "${e.title}_${eventStart.millisecondsSinceEpoch}";
          if (uniqueSignatures.contains(signature)) continue;
          uniqueSignatures.add(signature);

          externalEvents.add({
            'type': 'external_event',
            'time': eventStart,
            'endTime': eventEnd, // 笘・霑ｽ蜉
            'title': e.title ?? '莠亥ｮ・,
            'subtitle': e.location ?? 'OS繧ｫ繝ｬ繝ｳ繝繝ｼ繧医ｊ',
            'status': 0,
            'isAllDay': isAllDay,
          });
        }
      }
    }
    return externalEvents;
  }
}
import 'lms_adapter.dart'; // 笘・currentLMS 繧定ｪｭ縺ｿ霎ｼ繧
//course_parser.dart

class CourseTitleParser {
  static String getCleanTitle(String rawName) => currentLMS.cleanTitle(rawName);
}
class CourseScheduleParser {
  static String getRoomNumber(String rawName) => currentLMS.extractRoom(rawName);
  static String getDayPeriod(String rawName) => currentLMS.extractDayPeriod(rawName);
}

class CourseInstructorParser {
  static String getInstructorName(dynamic course) {
    try {
      if (course['teachers'] != null && (course['teachers'] as List).isNotEmpty) return course['teachers'][0]['display_name'] ?? '';
      return '';
    } catch (_) { return ''; }
  }
}
import 'dart:core';
//lms_adapter.dart

// ============================================================================
// 笘・譁ｰ隕剰ｿｽ蜉: LMS Adapter Pattern (螟ｧ蟄ｦ萓晏ｭ倥Ο繧ｸ繝・け縺ｮ髫秘屬繝ｬ繧､繝､繝ｼ)
// ============================================================================
abstract class LMSAdapter {
  String get baseUrl;

  // 譁・ｭ怜・繝代・繧ｹ邉ｻ
  String cleanTitle(String rawName);
  String extractRoom(String rawName);
  String extractDayPeriod(String rawName);
  List<Map<String, int>> parseWeeklySchedule(String rawName);

  // 蟄ｦ譛溘・蟷ｴ蠎ｦ蛻､螳夂ｳｻ
  bool isSpringSemester(DateTime now);
  int getDefaultYear(DateTime now);
  String getDefaultTerm(DateTime now);
  bool isTargetCourse(dynamic course, int selectedYear, String selectedTerm);

  // 譛医・繧ｹ繧ｱ繧ｸ繝･繝ｼ繝ｫ縺ｮ譛滄俣蛻､螳夂ｳｻ
  bool isSpringMonth(int month);
  bool isFallMonth(int month);
  bool isSpringCourse(String combinedName);
  bool isFallCourse(String combinedName);
  bool isFullYearCourse(String combinedName);

  DateTime getSemesterStartDate(DateTime now, bool isSpring);
  DateTime getSemesterEndDate(DateTime now, bool isSpring);
}

class KeioAdapter implements LMSAdapter {
  @override
  String get baseUrl => 'https://lms.keio.jp';

  @override
  String cleanTitle(String rawName) {
    try {
      String name = rawName.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\[.*?\]'), '').trim();
      if (name.contains('縲')) {
        final parts = name.split('縲');
        if (parts.isNotEmpty) return parts.last.trim();
      }
      return name;
    } catch (_) { return rawName; }
  }

  @override
  String extractRoom(String rawName) {
    try {
      final match = RegExp(r'[\[・ｻ]([^\[\]・ｻ・ｽ]+)[\]・ｽ]$').firstMatch(rawName.trim());
      return match != null ? (match.group(1) ?? "").trim() : "";
    } catch (_) { return ""; }
  }

  @override
  String extractDayPeriod(String rawName) {
    try {
      final match = RegExp(r'[\[・ｻ]([譛育↓豌ｴ譛ｨ驥大悄\s1-7・・・・・珪+)[\]・ｽ]').firstMatch(rawName);
      if (match != null) {
        String content = match.group(1)!;
        final pairs = RegExp(r'(譛・轣ｫ|豌ｴ|譛ｨ|驥掃蝨・\s*([1-7・・・余)').allMatches(content);
        if (pairs.isNotEmpty) {
          const dayMap = {'譛・:'MON','轣ｫ':'TUE','豌ｴ':'WED','譛ｨ':'THU','驥・:'FRI','蝨・:'SAT'};
          const pMap = {'・・:'1','・・:'2','・・:'3','・・:'4','・・:'5','・・:'6','・・:'7'};
          return pairs.map((p) => "${dayMap[p.group(1)!] ?? p.group(1)!} ${pMap[p.group(2)!] ?? p.group(2)!}").join(' / ');
        }
      }
      return "";
    } catch (_) { return ""; }
  }

  @override
  List<Map<String, int>> parseWeeklySchedule(String rawName) {
    List<Map<String, int>> schedule = [];
    final match = RegExp(r'\[([譛育↓豌ｴ譛ｨ驥大悄\s1-7・・・・・珪+)\]').firstMatch(rawName);
    if (match != null) {
      final pairs = RegExp(r'(譛・轣ｫ|豌ｴ|譛ｨ|驥掃蝨・\s*([1-7・・・余)').allMatches(match.group(1)!);
      for (var p in pairs) {
        int day = ['譛・, '轣ｫ', '豌ｴ', '譛ｨ', '驥・, '蝨・].indexOf(p.group(1)!);
        int period = (int.tryParse(p.group(2)!) ?? 1) - 1;
        if (day >= 0 && period >= 0 && period < 6) schedule.add({'day': day, 'period': period});
      }
    }
    return schedule;
  }

  @override
  bool isSpringSemester(DateTime now) {
    return (now.month >= 4 && now.month <= 8) || (now.month == 9 && now.day <= 1);
  }

  @override
  int getDefaultYear(DateTime now) {
    bool isSpring = isSpringSemester(now);
    return (!isSpring && now.month < 4) ? now.year - 1 : now.year;
  }

  @override
  String getDefaultTerm(DateTime now) {
    return isSpringSemester(now) ? "譏･蟄ｦ譛・ : "遘句ｭｦ譛・;
  }

  @override
  bool isTargetCourse(dynamic course, int selectedYear, String selectedTerm) {
    String courseName = course['name'] ?? '';
    String courseCode = course['course_code'] ?? '';
    String termName = course['term']?['name'] ?? '';
    String combined = "$courseName $courseCode $termName".toUpperCase();

    // 1. 蟷ｴ蠎ｦ縺ｮ繝√ぉ繝・け
    String fullYear = selectedYear.toString();
    String shortYear = (selectedYear % 100).toString();
    if (!combined.contains(fullYear) && !combined.contains(shortYear)) return false;

    // 2. 縲碁壼ｹｴ縲阪・繝√ぉ繝・け・亥ｸｸ縺ｫ陦ｨ遉ｺ・・
    if (combined.contains("騾壼ｹｴ") || combined.contains("FULL YEAR")) return true;

    // 3. 蝓ｺ譛ｬ縺ｨ縺ｪ繧句ｭｦ譛溘・蛻､螳・
    bool isSpringCourse = combined.contains("譏･") || combined.contains("蜑肴悄") || combined.contains("SPRING");
    bool isFallCourse = combined.contains("遘・) || combined.contains("蠕梧悄") || combined.contains("FALL");

    // 4. 繧ｯ繧ｩ繝ｼ繧ｿ繝ｼ・亥燕蜊・蠕悟濠・峨・蠑ｷ蜉帙↑蛻､螳・
    // 窶ｻ縲悟ｾｮ遨榊・隨ｬ1縲阪↑縺ｩ縺ｮ遘醍岼蜷阪↓隱､辷・＠縺ｪ縺・ｈ縺・檎ｬｬ1縲榊腰菴薙〒縺ｯ縺ｪ縺上・Q縲阪檎ｬｬ1繧ｯ繧ｩ繝ｼ繧ｿ繝ｼ縲咲ｭ峨〒蜴ｳ蟇・↓諡ｾ縺・∪縺・
    bool isQ1 = combined.contains("Q1") || combined.contains("1Q") || combined.contains("隨ｬ1繧ｯ繧ｩ繝ｼ繧ｿ繝ｼ") || combined.contains("譏･蟄ｦ譛溷燕蜊・);
    bool isQ2 = combined.contains("Q2") || combined.contains("2Q") || combined.contains("隨ｬ2繧ｯ繧ｩ繝ｼ繧ｿ繝ｼ") || combined.contains("譏･蟄ｦ譛溷ｾ悟濠");
    bool isQ3 = combined.contains("Q3") || combined.contains("3Q") || combined.contains("隨ｬ3繧ｯ繧ｩ繝ｼ繧ｿ繝ｼ") || combined.contains("遘句ｭｦ譛溷燕蜊・);
    bool isQ4 = combined.contains("Q4") || combined.contains("4Q") || combined.contains("隨ｬ4繧ｯ繧ｩ繝ｼ繧ｿ繝ｼ") || combined.contains("遘句ｭｦ譛溷ｾ悟濠");

    // ・域舞貂域蒔鄂ｮ・峨ｂ縺励悟燕蜊翫阪悟ｾ悟濠縲阪→縺・≧譁・ｭ励′蜷ｫ縺ｾ繧後※縺・※縲∵丼縺狗ｧ九°縺悟愛譏弱＠縺ｦ縺・ｋ蝣ｴ蜷・
    if (combined.contains("蜑榊濠")) {
      if (isSpringCourse) isQ1 = true;
      if (isFallCourse) isQ3 = true;
    }
    if (combined.contains("蠕悟濠")) {
      if (isSpringCourse) isQ2 = true;
      if (isFallCourse) isQ4 = true;
    }

    // 5. 繝輔Ν繧ｻ繝｡繧ｹ繧ｿ繝ｼ・亥ｭｦ譛溷・菴難ｼ峨°縺ｩ縺・°縺ｮ蛻､螳・
    // 譏･蟄ｦ譛溘□縺代←縲＿1縺ｧ繧２2縺ｧ繧ゅ↑縺・・・縲梧丼蟄ｦ譛溘・蜊雁ｹｴ髢薙★縺｣縺ｨ縺ゅｋ謗域･ｭ・医ヵ繝ｫ繧ｻ繝｡繧ｹ繧ｿ繝ｼ・峨・
    bool isFullSpring = isSpringCourse && !isQ1 && !isQ2;
    // 遘句ｭｦ譛溘□縺代←縲＿3縺ｧ繧２4縺ｧ繧ゅ↑縺・・・縲檎ｧ句ｭｦ譛溘・蜊雁ｹｴ髢薙★縺｣縺ｨ縺ゅｋ謗域･ｭ・医ヵ繝ｫ繧ｻ繝｡繧ｹ繧ｿ繝ｼ・峨・
    bool isFullFall = isFallCourse && !isQ3 && !isQ4;

    // 6. 繝ｦ繝ｼ繧ｶ繝ｼ縺ｮ驕ｸ謚橸ｼ・electedTerm・峨↓蝓ｺ縺･縺・◆繝輔ぅ繝ｫ繧ｿ繝ｪ繝ｳ繧ｰ

    // --- 譏･蟄ｦ譛溽ｳｻ ---
    if (selectedTerm == "譏･蟄ｦ譛・) {
      // 譏･蟄ｦ譛溷・菴薙ｒ驕ｸ縺ｶ縺ｨ縲√群1縲阪群2縲阪梧丼蟄ｦ譛溷・菴薙阪・蜈ｨ縺ｦ繧定｡ｨ遉ｺ
      return isFullSpring || isQ1 || isQ2;
    }
    if (selectedTerm.contains("Q1") && !selectedTerm.contains("Q2")) {
      // Q1縺ｮ縺ｿ驕ｸ謚樊凾・壹群1縲阪→縲梧丼蟄ｦ譛溷・菴薙阪ｒ陦ｨ遉ｺ・・2蟆ら畑縺ｯ髱櫁｡ｨ遉ｺ・・
      return isQ1 || isFullSpring;
    }
    if (selectedTerm.contains("Q2") && !selectedTerm.contains("Q1")) {
      // Q2縺ｮ縺ｿ驕ｸ謚樊凾・壹群2縲阪→縲梧丼蟄ｦ譛溷・菴薙阪ｒ陦ｨ遉ｺ・・1蟆ら畑縺ｯ髱櫁｡ｨ遉ｺ・・
      return isQ2 || isFullSpring;
    }
    if (selectedTerm.contains("Q1") && selectedTerm.contains("Q2")) {
      return isFullSpring || isQ1 || isQ2;
    }

    // --- 遘句ｭｦ譛溽ｳｻ ---
    if (selectedTerm == "遘句ｭｦ譛・) {
      return isFullFall || isQ3 || isQ4;
    }
    if (selectedTerm.contains("Q3") && !selectedTerm.contains("Q4")) {
      // Q3縺ｮ縺ｿ驕ｸ謚樊凾・壹群3縲阪→縲檎ｧ句ｭｦ譛溷・菴薙阪ｒ陦ｨ遉ｺ・・4蟆ら畑縺ｯ髱櫁｡ｨ遉ｺ・・
      return isQ3 || isFullFall;
    }
    if (selectedTerm.contains("Q4") && !selectedTerm.contains("Q3")) {
      // Q4縺ｮ縺ｿ驕ｸ謚樊凾・壹群4縲阪→縲檎ｧ句ｭｦ譛溷・菴薙阪ｒ陦ｨ遉ｺ・・3蟆ら畑縺ｯ髱櫁｡ｨ遉ｺ・・
      return isQ4 || isFullFall;
    }
    if (selectedTerm.contains("Q3") && selectedTerm.contains("Q4")) {
      return isFullFall || isQ3 || isQ4;
    }

    // 縺ｩ繧後↓繧ょｽ薙※縺ｯ縺ｾ繧峨↑縺・ｴ蜷茨ｼ医き繧ｹ繧ｿ繝縺ｧ螟峨↑譁・ｭ励′謖・ｮ壹＆繧後◆譎ゅ・菫晞匱・・
    return combined.contains(selectedTerm);
  }

  @override
  bool isSpringMonth(int month) => (month >= 4 && month <= 7);

  @override
  bool isFallMonth(int month) => (month >= 10 || month == 1);

  @override
  bool isSpringCourse(String combinedName) => combinedName.contains('譏･') || combinedName.contains('蜑肴悄') || combinedName.contains('Q1') || combinedName.contains('Q2');

  @override
  bool isFallCourse(String combinedName) => combinedName.contains('遘・) || combinedName.contains('蠕梧悄') || combinedName.contains('Q3') || combinedName.contains('Q4');

  @override
  bool isFullYearCourse(String combinedName) => combinedName.contains('騾壼ｹｴ');

  @override
  DateTime getSemesterStartDate(DateTime now, bool isSpring) {
    return isSpring ? DateTime(now.year, 4, 1) : DateTime(now.month < 4 ? now.year - 1 : now.year, 9, 20);
  }

  @override
  DateTime getSemesterEndDate(DateTime now, bool isSpring) {
    return isSpring ? DateTime(now.year, 7, 31) : DateTime(now.month < 4 ? now.year : now.year + 1, 1, 31);
  }
}

// 笘・繧ｰ繝ｭ繝ｼ繝舌Ν縺ｧ迴ｾ蝨ｨ縺ｮ繧｢繝繝励ち繝ｼ繧貞ｮ夂ｾｩ・医％繧後ｒ蛻・ｊ譖ｿ縺医ｋ縺縺代〒莉門､ｧ蟄ｦ縺ｫ蟇ｾ蠢懷庄閭ｽ縺ｫ縺ｪ繧翫∪縺呻ｼ・
final LMSAdapter currentLMS = KeioAdapter();
String get canvasBaseUrl => currentLMS.baseUrl;
// ============================================================================
